Excellent! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile my comprehensive findings:

## Comprehensive Notification System Analysis

I've thoroughly explored the Changelogue notification system. Here's your complete reference for designing an email notification channel:

### 1. Current Notification Channels

**Implemented:**
- **Slack** (`SlackSender`) - Slack Block Kit messages via incoming webhooks
- **Discord** (`DiscordSender`) - Discord embeds via webhook
- **Webhook** (`WebhookSender`) - Generic JSON POST to custom URLs

**File Location:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/`

### 2. Notification Interface & Architecture

**The Core Interface (all channels must implement):**

```go
type Sender interface {
    Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error
}
```

**Universal Notification Payload:**
```go
type Notification struct {
    Title       string // Project name or "Release Title"
    Body        string // Raw release JSON or SemanticReport JSON
    Version     string // e.g., "v1.21.0-rc.1"
    ProjectName string
    Provider    string // 'github', 'dockerhub', 'ecr-public', 'gitlab'
    Repository  string // e.g., "example/project"
    ReleaseURL  string // Internal Changelogue link
    SourceURL   string // Upstream provider link
}
```

**Configuration Format:** Each channel stores provider-specific config as JSONB in the database. For email, you'd define:
```go
type emailConfig struct {
    SMTPHost    string `json:"smtp_host"`
    SMTPPort    int    `json:"smtp_port"`
    Username    string `json:"username"`
    Password    string `json:"password"`    // or reference to secrets manager
    FromAddress string `json:"from_address"`
    ToAddresses []string `json:"to_addresses"` // or single recipient_email
}
```

### 3. Notification Preferences Storage

**Database Schema (PostgreSQL):**

```sql
notification_channels (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL        -- 'slack', 'discord', 'webhook', 'email'
    config JSONB NOT NULL            -- provider-specific config
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ
)

subscriptions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY
    channel_id UUID REFERENCES notification_channels(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release'))
    source_id UUID REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE    -- for source-level subscriptions
    project_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE  -- for project-level subscriptions
    version_filter TEXT                -- optional regex to filter versions
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ
    
    CONSTRAINT channels_check (
        (type = 'source_release' AND source_id IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL)
        OR (type = 'semantic_release' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id IS NULL)
    )
)
```

**Key Points:**
- Channels are reusable (one channel can be referenced by multiple subscriptions)
- Subscriptions have two granularity levels:
  - **Source-level** (`source_release`): triggered on raw release detection, sends to subscribers of that specific ingestion source
  - **Project-level** (`semantic_release`): triggered on AI-generated semantic release completion, sends to subscribers of the project
- Version filtering happens at the subscription level via regex patterns

### 4. Notification Routing Flow

**End-to-End Data Flow:**
```
1. Ingestion Layer detects new release (file: internal/ingestion/)
   ↓
2. River job enqueued: NotifyJobArgs{release_id, source_id} 
   (file: internal/queue/jobs.go)
   ↓
3. NotifyWorker picks up job (file: internal/routing/worker.go)
   ↓
4. Worker.Work():
   a) Fetches release data
   b) Applies version filters (regex include/exclude, prerelease filtering)
   c) Queries: ListSourceSubscriptions(sourceID) 
      → returns all subscriptions pointing to that source
   d) For each subscription:
      - Gets the NotificationChannel
      - Looks up Sender by ch.Type (maps to senders map)
      - Calls sender.Send(ctx, channel, notification)
   e) Checks agent_rules for auto-triggering semantic analysis
   ↓
5. Sender implementation handles delivery
   (Each sender parses config, formats payload, POSTs with 10s timeout)
```

**Job Definition:**
```go
type NotifyJobArgs struct {
    ReleaseID string // UUID of release record
    SourceID  string // UUID of source (for subscription lookup)
}
```

### 5. How Existing Channels Are Implemented

**Example: Slack Implementation** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/slack.go`)

Key patterns:
1. **Parse config:** Unmarshal `ch.Config` JSONB into a type-specific struct
2. **Detect message type:** Try to parse `msg.Body` as `SemanticReport` JSON:
   - If successful: Build rich formatted blocks (Slack Block Kit)
   - If fails: Fallback to source-level notification with raw changelog
3. **Format payload:** Convert to provider-specific format (Slack Block Kit JSON)
4. **Send:** HTTP POST with 10-second timeout, check for 4xx/5xx errors
5. **Error handling:** Return error to River (job will retry)

**Slack-Specific Features:**
- Tries to parse body as `SemanticReport` for rich AI-generated content
- Converts Markdown to ASCII with regex rules
- Slack Block Kit headers, sections, context for semantic releases
- Slack attachments with auto-collapse for long changelogs
- Urgency emoji and color coding (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/CRITICAL)

### 6. Channel Registration & Wiring

**Where Senders Are Registered:**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go`

```go
func NewSenders() map[string]Sender {
    return map[string]Sender{
        "webhook": &WebhookSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
        "slack":   &SlackSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
        "discord": &DiscordSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    }
}
```

**Server Initialization:**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/server.go`

```go
channels := NewChannelsHandler(deps.ChannelsStore, routing.NewSenders())
```

To add email:
1. Create `EmailSender` struct in `routing/email.go`
2. Add `"email": &EmailSender{...}` to `NewSenders()` map
3. No code changes needed elsewhere (it's automatically picked up)

### 7. API Endpoints for Managing Notification Settings

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/channels.go`

| Endpoint | Method | Purpose |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `/api/v1/channels` | GET | List all channels (paginated) |
| `/api/v1/channels` | POST | Create a new channel |
| `/api/v1/channels/{id}` | GET | Get a specific channel |
| `/api/v1/channels/{id}` | PUT | Update channel config |
| `/api/v1/channels/{id}` | DELETE | Delete a channel |
| `/api/v1/channels/{id}/test` | POST | Send test notification |

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/subscriptions.go`

| Endpoint | Method | Purpose |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `/api/v1/subscriptions` | GET | List all subscriptions |
| `/api/v1/subscriptions` | POST | Create subscription (links channel to source/project) |
| `/api/v1/subscriptions/{id}` | GET | Get specific subscription |
| `/api/v1/subscriptions/{id}` | PUT | Update subscription |
| `/api/v1/subscriptions/{id}` | DELETE | Delete subscription |

**Create Channel Request Body:**
```json
{
  "name": "Company Email Alerts",
  "type": "email",
  "config": {
    "smtp_host": "smtp.example.com",
    "smtp_port": 587,
    "username": "alerts@example.com",
    "password": "secret",
    "from_address": "no-reply@example.com",
    "to_addresses": ["team@example.com", "ops@example.com"]
  }
}
```

**Create Source-Level Subscription Request Body:**
```json
{
  "channel_id": "uuid-of-email-channel",
  "type": "source_release",
  "source_id": "uuid-of-source",
  "version_filter": "^v[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+$"
}
```

**Create Project-Level Subscription Request Body:**
```json
{
  "channel_id": "uuid-of-email-channel",
  "type": "semantic_release",
  "project_id": "uuid-of-project"
}
```

**Test Channel Endpoint (`POST /channels/{id}/test`):**
- Sends a sample notification to verify the channel works
- Creates a mock `Notification` with sample GitHub release data
- Returns `{"status": "ok"}` on success or error details on failure

### 8. Error Handling & Delivery Guarantees

**Delivery Guarantees (Transactional Outbox Pattern):**
- Release insert + NotifyJobArgs enqueue happen in same transaction
- Using River's `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` for exactly-once processing
- Dead-letter queue (DLQ) for jobs that fail after max retries

**Error Handling in Notification Worker:**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go`

```go
// Store errors logged but don't fail the job
for _, sub := range subs {
    ch, err := w.store.GetChannel(ctx, sub.ChannelID)
    if err != nil {
        slog.Error("get channel failed", "channel_id", sub.ChannelID, "err", err)
        continue  // Skip this subscription
    }
    
    sender, ok := w.senders[ch.Type]
    if !ok {
        slog.Warn("unknown channel type", "type", ch.Type)
        continue  // Skip unknown types
    }
    
    if err := sender.Send(ctx, ch, msg); err != nil {
        slog.Error("send notification failed", "channel", ch.Name, "err", err)
        // Don't fail the job - other subs still get notified
    }
}
```

**Key Pattern:** Individual channel failures don't fail the entire job. Other subscriptions still receive their notifications (partial delivery is acceptable).

### 9. Supporting Utilities & Helpers

**Markdown Sanitization:**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/sender.go`

The system provides a `markdownToASCII()` function that:
- Converts GitHub PR links `#NNN (url)`
- Converts Markdown links `[text](url)` → `text (url)`
- Converts headings to ASCII art with dashes
- Strips bold, inline code, HTML tags
- Collapses 3+ blank lines to 2

This can be reused for email body formatting.

**Provider URL Builder:**
```go
ProviderURL(provider, repository, version) // Returns upstream link
ProviderLabel(provider)                     // Returns human-readable label
```

**Config Type Safety:**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/channels.go`
- Validates that `type` field is not empty
- Validates that `name` field is not empty
- Validates that `config` is not null/empty (must be valid JSON)

**Test Notification Payload** (from `channels.go` Test endpoint):

```

## Summary for Email Implementation

You have all the building blocks:

1. **Sender interface** (`Send(ctx, ch, msg) error`) - implement this
2. **Config schema** - define your SMTP parameters in JSONB
3. **Message handling** - support both raw releases and SemanticReport JSON bodies
4. **Error handling** - return errors for retries, individual failures ok
5. **Auto-wiring** - just add to `NewSenders()` map, no other changes needed
6. **API endpoints** - already exist for CRUD, test, subscription management
7. **Database layer** - ready to persist email channel configs and subscriptions
8. **Utilities** - `markdownToASCII()`, `ProviderURL()`, `ProviderLabel()` available for formatting

The system is plugin-friendly by design. You're ready to implement the email sender!

Great, requirements are clear. Let me now propose approaches.

## Approaches

Given the plugin-friendly architecture, this is fairly straightforward — the main decision is implementation approach for the HTML email rendering:

### Approach A: Go `html/template` (Recommended)

- Use Go's built-in `html/template` to render HTML emails
- Embed the template via `//go:embed`
- Multipart MIME: HTML + plain text (using `markdownToASCII()` for text part)
- Use Go's `net/smtp` with STARTTLS for delivery
- **Pros:** Zero dependencies, consistent with codebase style, fast
- **Cons:** HTML templates can be verbose

### Approach B: Inline HTML string builder

- Build HTML strings directly in Go code (like the Slack Block Kit builder does)
- No template files needed
- **Pros:** Keep everything in one file, easy to understand
- **Cons:** Harder to maintain HTML layout, no separation of concerns

### Approach C: External email library (e.g. `jordan-wright/email` or `go-mail/mail`)

- Use a third-party Go email library for MIME assembly
- **Pros:** Cleaner MIME handling, attachment support
- **Cons:** New dependency for a simple task; Go's stdlib is sufficient

**My recommendation: Approach A** — `html/template` with `//go:embed`. It follows the project's pattern of embedding static assets, keeps HTML maintainable, and requires zero new dependencies. The Slack/Discord senders already build structured content inline, but email HTML is complex enough to warrant a template.

Does Approach A sound good, or would you prefer a different one?

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    12→)
    13→
    14→type slackConfig struct {
    15→	WebhookURL string `json:"webhook_url"`
    16→}
    17→
    18→// SlackSender sends notifications as Slack Block Kit messages via incoming webhook.
    19→type SlackSender struct {
    20→	Client *http.Client
    21→}
    22→
    23→// slackPayload is the Slack incoming webhook payload using Block Kit and/or attachments.
    24→type slackPayload struct {
    25→	Blocks      []slackBlock      `json:"blocks,omitempty"`
    26→	Attachments []slackAttachment `json:"attachments,omitempty"`
    27→}
    28→
    29→type slackBlock struct {
    30→	Type     string      `json:"type"`
    31→	Text     *slackText  `json:"text,omitempty"`
    32→	Fields   []slackText `json:"fields,omitempty"`
    33→	Elements []slackText `json:"elements,omitempty"`
    34→}
    35→
    36→type slackText struct {
    37→	Type string `json:"type"`
    38→	Text string `json:"text"`
    39→}
    40→
    41→// slackAttachment uses Slack's legacy attachment format which auto-collapses
    42→// long text with a "Show more" button.
    43→type slackAttachment struct {
    44→	Color     string   `json:"color,omitempty"`
    45→	Title     string   `json:"title,omitempty"`
    46→	TitleLink string   `json:"title_link,omitempty"`
    47→	Text      string   `json:"text,omitempty"`
    48→	MrkdwnIn  []string `json:"mrkdwn_in,omitempty"`
    49→}
    50→
    51→// urgencyEmoji returns an emoji indicator for the given urgency level.
    52→func urgencyEmoji(level string) string {
    53→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
    54→	case "LOW":
    55→		return "🟢"
    56→	case "MEDIUM":
    57→		return "🟡"
    58→	case "HIGH":
    59→		return "🔴"
    60→	case "CRITICAL":
    61→		return "⚫"
    62→	default:
    63→		return "⚪"
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// buildSemanticBlocks builds compact Slack Block Kit blocks from a SemanticReport.
    68→// For Critical/High urgency, includes the urgency reason. For Low/Medium, omits it.
    69→func buildSemanticBlocks(title string, report *models.SemanticReport, msg Notification) []slackBlock {
    70→	// Resolve urgency from new field or backward-compat risk_level
    71→	urgency := report.Urgency
    72→	if urgency == "" {
    73→		urgency = report.RiskLevel
    74→	}
    75→	urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
    76→	if urgencyReason == "" {
    77→		urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	// Header: "ProjectName vX.Y.Z — 🟢 Low Urgency"
    81→	headerText := title
    82→	if urgency != "" {
    83→		headerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s — %s %s Urgency", title, urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)
    84→	}
    85→	// Slack header max is 150 chars
    86→	if len(headerText) > 150 {
    87→		headerText = headerText[:147] + "..."
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	blocks := []slackBlock{
    91→		{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: headerText}},
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	// Urgency reason (only for Critical/High)
    95→	upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
    96→	if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
    97→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
    98→			Type: "section",
    99→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("⚠️ %s", urgencyReason)},
   100→		})
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	// Changelog summary
   104→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
   105→	if summary == "" {
   106→		summary = report.Summary
   107→	}
   108→	if summary != "" {
   109→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   110→			Type: "section",
   111→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: summary},
   112→		})
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	// First download command only
   116→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   117→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   118→			Type: "section",
   119→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("`%s`", report.DownloadCommands[0])},
   120→		})
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	// Footer context with source info and links
   124→	var footerParts []string
   125→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   126→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository))
   127→	}
   128→	if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   129→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   130→	}
   131→	if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   132→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   133→	}
   134→	if len(footerParts) > 0 {
   135→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   136→			Type: "context",
   137→			Elements: []slackText{
   138→				{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: strings.Join(footerParts, "  |  ")},
   139→			},
   140→		})
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	return blocks
   144→}
   145→
   146→func (s *SlackSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   147→	var cfg slackConfig
   148→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   149→		return fmt.Errorf("parse slack config: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	// Try to parse body as a SemanticReport for rich formatting.
   153→	var payload slackPayload
   154→	var report models.SemanticReport
   155→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   156→		payload.Blocks = buildSemanticBlocks(msg.Title, &report, msg)
   157→	} else {
   158→		// Fallback: source-level release notification.
   159→		// Use attachments for changelog so Slack auto-collapses long text
   160→		// with a "Show more" button.
   161→		var changelogText string
   162→		if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok {
   163→			changelogText = fields.Changelog
   164→		}
   165→
   166→		if changelogText != "" {
   167→			// Build title link label
   168→			titleLabel := msg.Title
   169→			if msg.Repository != "" && msg.Provider != "" {
   170→				titleLabel = fmt.Sprintf("%s on %s", msg.Repository, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider))
   171→			}
   172→
   173→			// Build attachment text: title + version badge, then changelog
   174→			// converted to ASCII and wrapped in a code block. Slack
   175→			// auto-collapses long code blocks in attachments with a
   176→			// "Show more" button.
   177→			asciiChangelog := markdownToASCII(changelogText)
   178→			attachText := fmt.Sprintf("*%s* `%s`\n```%s```", msg.Title, msg.Version, asciiChangelog)
   179→
   180→			// Append links after the code block
   181→			var linkParts []string
   182→			if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   183→				linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   184→			}
   185→			if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   186→				linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   187→			}
   188→			if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   189→				attachText += "\n" + strings.Join(linkParts, "  |  ")
   190→			}
   191→
   192→			attachment := slackAttachment{
   193→				Color:    "#D3D3D3",
   194→				Title:    titleLabel,
   195→				Text:     attachText,
   196→				MrkdwnIn: []string{"text"},
   197→			}
   198→			if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   199→				attachment.TitleLink = msg.SourceURL
   200→			}
   201→			payload.Attachments = []slackAttachment{attachment}
   202→		} else {
   203→			// No changelog — show a clean version announcement instead of raw JSON.
   204→			headerText := msg.Title
   205→			if msg.Version != "" && msg.Title != msg.Version {
   206→				headerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", msg.Title, msg.Version)
   207→			}
   208→			if len(headerText) > 150 {
   209→				headerText = headerText[:147] + "..."
   210→			}
   211→
   212→			blocks := []slackBlock{
   213→				{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: headerText}},
   214→			}
   215→
   216→			var linkParts []string
   217→			if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   218→				linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository))
   219→			}
   220→			if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   221→				linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   222→			}
   223→			if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   224→				linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   225→			}
   226→			if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   227→				blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   228→					Type: "context",
   229→					Elements: []slackText{
   230→						{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: strings.Join(linkParts, "  |  ")},
   231→					},
   232→				})
   233→			}
   234→			payload.Blocks = blocks
   235→		}
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal slack payload: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
   244→	if err != nil {
   245→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   248→
   249→	client := s.Client
   250→	if client == nil {
   251→		client = http.DefaultClient
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		return fmt.Errorf("send slack notification: %w", err)
   257→	}
   258→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   259→
   260→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   261→		return fmt.Errorf("slack returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
   262→	}
   263→	return nil
   264→}
   265→

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"net/url"
     8→	"regexp"
     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Notification is the payload sent through all notification channels.
    15→type Notification struct {
    16→	Title       string `json:"title"`
    17→	Body        string `json:"body"`
    18→	Version     string `json:"version"`
    19→	ProjectName string `json:"project_name,omitempty"`
    20→	Provider    string `json:"provider,omitempty"`
    21→	Repository  string `json:"repository,omitempty"`
    22→	ReleaseURL  string `json:"release_url,omitempty"`  // internal Changelogue link
    23→	SourceURL   string `json:"source_url,omitempty"`   // upstream provider link
    24→}
    25→
    26→// Sender is the interface that all notification channel implementations must satisfy.
    27→type Sender interface {
    28→	Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error
    29→}
    30→
    31→// rawBodyFields contains the human-readable fields extracted from raw release JSON.
    32→type rawBodyFields struct {
    33→	Changelog  string
    34→	ReleaseURL string
    35→}
    36→
    37→// parseRawBody extracts known fields from a raw JSON release body.
    38→func parseRawBody(body string) (rawBodyFields, bool) {
    39→	var raw map[string]interface{}
    40→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &raw); err != nil {
    41→		return rawBodyFields{}, false
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	fields := rawBodyFields{
    45→		Changelog:  stringField(raw, "changelog"),
    46→		ReleaseURL: stringField(raw, "release_url"),
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	if fields.Changelog == "" && fields.ReleaseURL == "" {
    50→		return fields, false
    51→	}
    52→	return fields, true
    53→}
    54→
    55→func stringField(m map[string]interface{}, key string) string {
    56→	v, _ := m[key].(string)
    57→	return v
    58→}
    59→
    60→// ProviderURL builds the upstream release page URL for a given provider.
    61→func ProviderURL(provider, repository, version string) string {
    62→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {
    63→	case "github":
    64→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://github.com/%s/releases/tag/%s", repository, version)
    65→	case "dockerhub":
    66→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://hub.docker.com/r/%s/tags?name=%s", repository, url.QueryEscape(version))
    67→	case "ecr-public":
    68→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gallery.ecr.aws/%s", repository)
    69→	case "gitlab":
    70→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gitlab.com/%s/-/releases/%s", repository, version)
    71→	default:
    72→		return ""
    73→	}
    74→}
    75→
    76→// ProviderLabel returns a human-readable label for a provider.
    77→func ProviderLabel(provider string) string {
    78→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {
    79→	case "github":
    80→		return "GitHub"
    81→	case "dockerhub":
    82→		return "Docker Hub"
    83→	case "ecr-public":
    84→		return "ECR Public"
    85→	case "gitlab":
    86→		return "GitLab"
    87→	default:
    88→		return provider
    89→	}
    90→}
    91→
    92→// Regex patterns for markdown-to-ASCII conversion.
    93→var (
    94→	// GitHub PR/issue URLs → #NNN ( url )
    95→	reGitHubPR = regexp.MustCompile(`https?://github\.com/([^/]+/[^/]+)/(pull|issues)/(\d+)`)
    96→	// Markdown links [text](url) → text ( url )
    97→	reMdLink = regexp.MustCompile(`\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)`)
    98→	// Headings ## Text → dashed ASCII box
    99→	reHeading = regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^#{1,6}\s+(.+)$`)
   100→	// Bold **text** and __text__
   101→	reBoldStar = regexp.MustCompile(`\*\*(.+?)\*\*`)
   102→	reBoldUnderscore = regexp.MustCompile(`__(.+?)__`)
   103→	// Inline code `text`
   104→	reInlineCode = regexp.MustCompile("`([^`]+)`")
   105→	// HTML tags
   106→	reHTML = regexp.MustCompile(`<[^>]+>`)
   107→	// Images ![alt](url)
   108→	reImage = regexp.MustCompile(`!\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]+\)`)
   109→)
   110→
   111→// markdownToASCII converts GitHub-flavored Markdown into clean ASCII text
   112→// suitable for display inside Slack/Discord code blocks.
   113→func markdownToASCII(md string) string {
   114→	s := md
   115→
   116→	// Strip images first (before link processing)
   117→	s = reImage.ReplaceAllString(s, "$1")
   118→
   119→	// Convert GitHub PR/issue URLs to #NNN ( url )
   120→	s = reGitHubPR.ReplaceAllString(s, "#$3 ( $0 )")
   121→
   122→	// Convert markdown links [text](url) → text ( url )
   123→	s = reMdLink.ReplaceAllString(s, "$1 ( $2 )")
   124→
   125→	// Convert headings to ASCII art with dashes
   126→	s = reHeading.ReplaceAllStringFunc(s, func(match string) string {
   127→		parts := reHeading.FindStringSubmatch(match)
   128→		title := strings.TrimSpace(parts[1])
   129→		dashes := strings.Repeat("-", len(title))
   130→		return dashes + "\n" + title + "\n" + dashes
   131→	})
   132→
   133→	// Strip bold markers
   134→	s = reBoldStar.ReplaceAllString(s, "$1")
   135→	s = reBoldUnderscore.ReplaceAllString(s, "$1")
   136→
   137→	// Strip inline code backticks
   138→	s = reInlineCode.ReplaceAllString(s, "$1")
   139→
   140→	// Strip HTML tags
   141→	s = reHTML.ReplaceAllString(s, "")
   142→
   143→	// Collapse 3+ consecutive blank lines into 2
   144→	reBlankLines := regexp.MustCompile(`\n{3,}`)
   145→	s = reBlankLines.ReplaceAllString(s, "\n\n")
   146→
   147→	return strings.TrimSpace(s)
   148→}
   149→

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"log/slog"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"regexp"
    10→	"strings"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    14→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    15→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    16→)
    17→
    18→// NotifyStore is the data access interface required by the notification worker.
    19→type NotifyStore interface {
    20→	GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error)
    21→	GetSource(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Source, error)
    22→	ListSourceSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, sourceID string) ([]models.Subscription, error)
    23→	GetChannel(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.NotificationChannel, error)
    24→	GetProject(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Project, error)
    25→	GetPreviousRelease(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, beforeVersion string) (*models.Release, error)
    26→	EnqueueAgentRun(ctx context.Context, projectID, trigger, version string) error
    27→}
    28→
    29→// NotifyWorker is a River worker that processes NotifyJobArgs.
    30→// It looks up the release, finds all source-level subscriptions, resolves
    31→// the notification channel for each, and dispatches via the appropriate sender.
    32→// After sending notifications, it checks the project's agent rules and
    33→// auto-triggers an agent run if the version criteria are met.
    34→type NotifyWorker struct {
    35→	river.WorkerDefaults[queue.NotifyJobArgs]
    36→	store     NotifyStore
    37→	senders   map[string]Sender
    38→	publicURL string // base URL for internal Changelogue links (e.g. "https://changelogue.example.com")
    39→}
    40→
    41→// NewSenders returns the default sender map for webhook, Slack, and Discord.
    42→func NewSenders() map[string]Sender {
    43→	return map[string]Sender{
    44→		"webhook": &WebhookSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    45→		"slack":   &SlackSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    46→		"discord": &DiscordSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    47→	}
    48→}
    49→
    50→// NewNotifyWorker creates a NotifyWorker with default senders for webhook, Slack, and Discord.
    51→func NewNotifyWorker(store NotifyStore, publicURL string) *NotifyWorker {
    52→	return &NotifyWorker{
    53→		store:     store,
    54→		senders:   NewSenders(),
    55→		publicURL: strings.TrimRight(publicURL, "/"),
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// VersionPassesFilter returns true if the version string passes the source's
    60→// include/exclude regex filters. When both are nil the version always passes.
    61→func VersionPassesFilter(version string, include, exclude *string) bool {
    62→	if include != nil && *include != "" {
    63→		matched, err := regexp.MatchString(*include, version)
    64→		if err != nil || !matched {
    65→			return false
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	if exclude != nil && *exclude != "" {
    69→		matched, err := regexp.MatchString(*exclude, version)
    70→		if err != nil || matched {
    71→			return false
    72→		}
    73→	}
    74→	return true
    75→}
    76→
    77→// Work processes a notify job: fetches the release, resolves subscriptions,
    78→// sends notifications to all matching channels, and checks agent rules to
    79→// auto-trigger agent runs when version criteria are met.
    80→func (w *NotifyWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.NotifyJobArgs]) error {
    81→	release, err := w.store.GetRelease(ctx, job.Args.ReleaseID)
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		return fmt.Errorf("get release: %w", err)
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	// Check source version filters — skip entirely if filtered out.
    87→	source, err := w.store.GetSource(ctx, job.Args.SourceID)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return fmt.Errorf("get source: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→	if !VersionPassesFilter(release.Version, source.VersionFilterInclude, source.VersionFilterExclude) {
    92→		slog.Debug("release filtered by version filter", "version", release.Version, "source_id", job.Args.SourceID)
    93→		return nil
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Check exclude_prereleases — skip if source excludes prereleases and this is one.
    97→	if source.ExcludePrereleases {
    98→		var rawData map[string]interface{}
    99→		if err := json.Unmarshal(release.RawData, &rawData); err == nil {
   100→			if prerelease, _ := rawData["prerelease"].(string); prerelease == "true" {
   101→				slog.Debug("release filtered by exclude_prereleases", "version", release.Version, "source_id", job.Args.SourceID)
   102→				return nil
   103→			}
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	subs, err := w.store.ListSourceSubscriptions(ctx, job.Args.SourceID)
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("list subscriptions: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	for _, sub := range subs {
   113→		ch, err := w.store.GetChannel(ctx, sub.ChannelID)
   114→		if err != nil {
   115→			slog.Error("get channel failed", "channel_id", sub.ChannelID, "err", err)
   116→			continue
   117→		}
   118→
   119→		sender, ok := w.senders[ch.Type]
   120→		if !ok {
   121→			slog.Warn("unknown channel type", "type", ch.Type)
   122→			continue
   123→		}
   124→
   125→		// Build title with project name if available.
   126→		title := release.Version
   127→		if release.ProjectName != "" {
   128→			title = release.ProjectName
   129→		}
   130→
   131→		msg := Notification{
   132→			Title:       title,
   133→			Body:        string(release.RawData),
   134→			Version:     release.Version,
   135→			ProjectName: release.ProjectName,
   136→			Provider:    release.Provider,
   137→			Repository:  release.Repository,
   138→			SourceURL:   ProviderURL(release.Provider, release.Repository, release.Version),
   139→		}
   140→		if w.publicURL != "" {
   141→			msg.ReleaseURL = fmt.Sprintf("%s/releases/%s", w.publicURL, release.ID)
   142→		}
   143→
   144→		if err := sender.Send(ctx, ch, msg); err != nil {
   145→			slog.Error("send notification failed", "channel", ch.Name, "err", err)
   146→		}
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Check agent rules and auto-trigger if criteria are met.
   150→	w.checkAgentRules(ctx, release, source)
   151→
   152→	return nil
   153→}
   154→
   155→// checkAgentRules evaluates the project's agent rules against the new release
   156→// and the previous release for this source. If any rule matches, an agent run
   157→// is enqueued. Errors are logged but do not fail the job — notification delivery
   158→// is the primary responsibility.
   159→func (w *NotifyWorker) checkAgentRules(ctx context.Context, release *models.Release, source *models.Source) {
   160→	project, err := w.store.GetProject(ctx, source.ProjectID)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		slog.Error("get project for agent rules", "project_id", source.ProjectID, "err", err)
   163→		return
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Parse the project's agent_rules JSON.
   167→	var rules models.AgentRules
   168→	if len(project.AgentRules) > 0 {
   169→		if err := json.Unmarshal(project.AgentRules, &rules); err != nil {
   170→			slog.Error("unmarshal agent rules", "project_id", project.ID, "err", err)
   171→			return
   172→		}
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Determine the previous version for comparison.
   176→	var previousVersion string
   177→	prev, err := w.store.GetPreviousRelease(ctx, source.ID, release.Version)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		slog.Error("get previous release for agent rules", "source_id", source.ID, "err", err)
   180→		return
   181→	}
   182→	if prev != nil {
   183→		previousVersion = prev.Version
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	if CheckAgentRules(&rules, release.Version, previousVersion) {
   187→		trigger := fmt.Sprintf("auto:version:%s", release.Version)
   188→		if err := w.store.EnqueueAgentRun(ctx, source.ProjectID, trigger, release.Version); err != nil {
   189→			slog.Error("enqueue agent run", "project_id", source.ProjectID, "err", err)
   190→		} else {
   191→			slog.Info("agent run triggered by version rules",
   192→				"project_id", source.ProjectID,
   193→				"version", release.Version,
   194→				"previous_version", previousVersion,
   195→			)
   196→		}
   197→	}
   198→}
   199→

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// discordEmbedDescriptionLimit is Discord's maximum embed description length.
    15→const discordEmbedDescriptionLimit = 4096
    16→
    17→type discordConfig struct {
    18→	WebhookURL string `json:"webhook_url"`
    19→}
    20→
    21→// DiscordSender sends notifications as Discord webhook messages with embeds.
    22→type DiscordSender struct {
    23→	Client *http.Client
    24→}
    25→
    26→// discordPayload is the Discord webhook payload.
    27→type discordPayload struct {
    28→	Embeds []discordEmbed `json:"embeds"`
    29→}
    30→
    31→type discordEmbed struct {
    32→	Title       string              `json:"title"`
    33→	URL         string              `json:"url,omitempty"`
    34→	Description string              `json:"description"`
    35→	Color       int                 `json:"color"`
    36→	Fields      []discordEmbedField `json:"fields,omitempty"`
    37→	Footer      *discordEmbedFooter `json:"footer,omitempty"`
    38→}
    39→
    40→type discordEmbedField struct {
    41→	Name   string `json:"name"`
    42→	Value  string `json:"value"`
    43→	Inline bool   `json:"inline"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→type discordEmbedFooter struct {
    47→	Text string `json:"text"`
    48→}
    49→
    50→// discordUrgencyColor returns a Discord embed color based on urgency level.
    51→func discordUrgencyColor(level string) int {
    52→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
    53→	case "CRITICAL":
    54→		return 0x111113 // near-black
    55→	case "HIGH":
    56→		return 0xDC2626 // red
    57→	case "MEDIUM":
    58→		return 0xD97706 // amber
    59→	case "LOW":
    60→		return 0x16A34A // green
    61→	default:
    62→		return 0x5865F2 // Discord blurple
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→// buildSemanticEmbed builds a compact Discord embed from a SemanticReport.
    67→func buildSemanticEmbed(title string, version string, report *models.SemanticReport, msg Notification) discordEmbed {
    68→	// Resolve urgency from new field or backward-compat risk_level
    69→	urgency := report.Urgency
    70→	if urgency == "" {
    71→		urgency = report.RiskLevel
    72→	}
    73→	urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
    74→	if urgencyReason == "" {
    75→		urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var descParts []string
    79→
    80→	// Urgency badge
    81→	if urgency != "" {
    82→		descParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s **%s Urgency**", urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency))
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	// Urgency reason (only for Critical/High)
    86→	upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
    87→	if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
    88→		descParts = append(descParts, urgencyReason)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Changelog summary
    92→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
    93→	if summary == "" {
    94→		summary = report.Summary
    95→	}
    96→	if summary != "" {
    97→		descParts = append(descParts, summary)
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// First download command
   101→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   102→		descParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf("`%s`", report.DownloadCommands[0]))
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// Links
   106→	var linkParts []string
   107→	if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   108→		linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View on %s](%s)", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.SourceURL))
   109→	}
   110→	if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   111→		linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View in Changelogue](%s)", msg.ReleaseURL))
   112→	}
   113→	if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   114→		descParts = append(descParts, strings.Join(linkParts, "  •  "))
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	description := strings.Join(descParts, "\n\n")
   118→	if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
   119→		description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	// Footer with source info
   123→	var footerText string
   124→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   125→		footerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	embed := discordEmbed{
   129→		Title:       title,
   130→		Description: description,
   131→		Color:       discordUrgencyColor(urgency),
   132→	}
   133→	if footerText != "" {
   134→		embed.Footer = &discordEmbedFooter{Text: footerText}
   135→	}
   136→	return embed
   137→}
   138→
   139→func (s *DiscordSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   140→	var cfg discordConfig
   141→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   142→		return fmt.Errorf("parse discord config: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	var embed discordEmbed
   146→	var report models.SemanticReport
   147→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   148→		embed = buildSemanticEmbed(msg.Title, msg.Version, &report, msg)
   149→	} else {
   150→		// Fallback: extract known fields from raw data for a readable message.
   151→		var descParts []string
   152→		if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok && fields.Changelog != "" {
   153→			// Convert changelog markdown to ASCII and wrap in a code
   154→			// block. Discord auto-collapses long code blocks.
   155→			asciiChangelog := markdownToASCII(fields.Changelog)
   156→			descParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf("```%s```", asciiChangelog))
   157→		}
   158→
   159→		// Add links
   160→		var linkParts []string
   161→		if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   162→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View on %s](%s)", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.SourceURL))
   163→		}
   164→		if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   165→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View in Changelogue](%s)", msg.ReleaseURL))
   166→		}
   167→		if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   168→			descParts = append(descParts, strings.Join(linkParts, "  •  "))
   169→		}
   170→
   171→		description := strings.Join(descParts, "\n\n")
   172→		if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
   173→			description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
   174→		}
   175→
   176→		embed = discordEmbed{
   177→			Title:       msg.Title,
   178→			Description: description,
   179→			Color:       0x5865F2,
   180→			Fields: []discordEmbedField{
   181→				{Name: "Version", Value: msg.Version, Inline: true},
   182→			},
   183→		}
   184→		if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   185→			embed.Footer = &discordEmbedFooter{Text: fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)}
   186→		}
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	payload := discordPayload{Embeds: []discordEmbed{embed}}
   190→
   191→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal discord payload: %w", err)
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   201→
   202→	client := s.Client
   203→	if client == nil {
   204→		client = http.DefaultClient
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   208→	if err != nil {
   209→		return fmt.Errorf("send discord notification: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   212→
   213→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   214→		return fmt.Errorf("discord returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
   215→	}
   216→	return nil
   217→}
   218→

    15→func TestSlackSender_Send(t *testing.T) {
    16→	var received []byte
    17→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    18→		if r.Header.Get("Content-Type") != "application/json" {
    19→			t.Errorf("expected Content-Type application/json, got %s", r.Header.Get("Content-Type"))
    20→		}
    21→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
    22→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
    23→	}))
    24→	defer srv.Close()
    25→
    26→	sender := &SlackSender{Client: srv.Client()}
    27→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
    28→		Type:   "slack",
    29→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
    30→	}
    31→	msg := Notification{
    32→		Title:      "geth",
    33→		Body:       `{"changelog":"Security fixes and performance improvements"}`,
    34→		Version:    "v1.14.0",
    35→		Provider:   "github",
    36→		Repository: "ethereum/go-ethereum",
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    42→	}
    43→	if len(received) == 0 {
    44→		t.Fatal("expected Slack to receive payload")
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Verify it's a valid Slack payload with attachments (has changelog).
    48→	var payload map[string]interface{}
    49→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
    50→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
    51→	}
    52→	attachments, ok := payload["attachments"]
    53→	if !ok {
    54→		t.Fatal("expected 'attachments' key in Slack payload")
    55→	}
    56→	attList, ok := attachments.([]interface{})
    57→	if !ok || len(attList) < 1 {
    58→		t.Fatalf("expected at least 1 attachment, got %v", attachments)
    59→	}
    60→}

    93→func TestSlackSender_SemanticReport(t *testing.T) {
    94→	var received []byte
    95→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    96→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
    97→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
    98→	}))
    99→	defer srv.Close()
   100→
   101→	sender := &SlackSender{Client: srv.Client()}
   102→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   103→		Type:   "slack",
   104→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	reportJSON := `{
   108→		"subject": "Ready to Deploy: go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   109→		"urgency": "High",
   110→		"urgency_reason": "Security vulnerability patched",
   111→		"status_checks": ["Binaries Unverified", "Docker Image Unverified"],
   112→		"changelog_summary": "Security fixes and performance improvements",
   113→		"availability": "GA",
   114→		"adoption": "Recommended for production",
   115→		"recommendation": "Deploy after verifying checksums",
   116→		"download_commands": ["docker pull ethereum/client-go:v1.16.4"],
   117→		"download_links": ["https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4"]
   118→	}`
   119→
   120→	msg := Notification{
   121→		Title:       "Semantic Release Report: go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   122→		Body:        reportJSON,
   123→		Version:     "v1.16.4",
   124→		ProjectName: "go-ethereum",
   125→		Provider:    "github",
   126→		Repository:  "ethereum/go-ethereum",
   127→		SourceURL:   "https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4",
   128→		ReleaseURL:  "https://changelogue.example.com/sr/1",
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   132→	if err != nil {
   133→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	var payload slackPayload
   137→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   138→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Compact format: header + urgency_reason (High) + changelog + download cmd + context = 5 blocks
   142→	if len(payload.Blocks) < 3 || len(payload.Blocks) > 6 {
   143→		t.Fatalf("expected 3-6 blocks for compact semantic report, got %d", len(payload.Blocks))
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// First block should be header containing project name, version, and urgency
   147→	if payload.Blocks[0].Type != "header" {
   148→		t.Fatalf("expected first block to be header, got %s", payload.Blocks[0].Type)
   149→	}
   150→	headerText := payload.Blocks[0].Text.Text
   151→	if !strings.Contains(headerText, "go-ethereum") || !strings.Contains(headerText, "v1.16.4") {
   152→		t.Fatalf("header should contain project name and version, got %q", headerText)
   153→	}
   154→	if !strings.Contains(headerText, "High") {
   155→		t.Fatalf("header should contain urgency level, got %q", headerText)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Should NOT have a fields section (no separate risk/urgency/availability fields)
   159→	for _, b := range payload.Blocks {
   160→		if len(b.Fields) > 0 {
   161→			t.Fatal("compact format should not have field sections")
   162→		}
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Should have download command
   166→	hasCode := false
   167→	for _, b := range payload.Blocks {
   168→		if b.Text != nil && strings.Contains(b.Text.Text, "docker pull") {
   169→			hasCode = true
   170→		}
   171→	}
   172→	if !hasCode {
   173→		t.Fatal("expected download command")
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// Last block should be context footer
   177→	lastBlock := payload.Blocks[len(payload.Blocks)-1]
   178→	if lastBlock.Type != "context" {
   179→		t.Fatalf("expected last block to be context, got %s", lastBlock.Type)
   180→	}
   181→}

   233→func TestSlackSender_NonReportFallback(t *testing.T) {
   234→	var received []byte
   235→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   236→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
   237→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)

   239→	defer srv.Close()

   241→	sender := &SlackSender{Client: srv.Client()}

   246→
   247→	// Plain text body (not a semantic report) should use simple fallback
   248→	msg := Notification{
   249→		Title:   "geth",
   250→		Body:    "Released on GitHub with security fixes",
   251→		Version: "v1.14.0",
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	var payload slackPayload
   260→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   261→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	// Fallback with no changelog should produce a header block (no raw JSON dumped)
   265→	if len(payload.Blocks) < 1 {
   266→		t.Fatal("expected at least 1 block for non-report")
   267→	}
   268→	if payload.Blocks[0].Type != "header" {
   269→		t.Fatalf("expected header block, got %s", payload.Blocks[0].Type)
   270→	}
   271→	// Should NOT contain raw body text in a section block
   272→	for _, b := range payload.Blocks {
   273→		if b.Type == "section" && b.Text != nil && b.Text.Text == "Released on GitHub with security fixes" {
   274→			t.Fatal("should not dump raw body text in section block")
   275→		}
   276→	}
   277→}
   278→
   279→func TestSlackSender_RawJSONFallback(t *testing.T) {
   280→	var received []byte
   281→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   282→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
   283→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
   284→	}))
   285→	defer srv.Close()
   286→
   287→	sender := &SlackSender{Client: srv.Client()}
   288→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   289→		Type:   "slack",
   290→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	// Raw release JSON with metadata on the Notification struct (as worker now provides)
   294→	msg := Notification{
   295→		Title:       "zkSync Era",
   296→		Body:        `{"changelog":"Fixed wrong genesis commit","prerelease":"false","release_url":"https://github.com/matter-labs/zksync-era/releases/tag/zkos-0.29.4-rc1"}`,
   297→		Version:     "zkos-0.29.4-rc1",
   298→		ProjectName: "zkSync Era",
   299→		Provider:    "github",
   300→		Repository:  "matter-labs/zksync-era",
   301→		SourceURL:   "https://github.com/matter-labs/zksync-era/releases/tag/zkos-0.29.4-rc1",
   302→		ReleaseURL:  "https://changelogue.example.com/releases/rel-1",
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   306→	if err != nil {
   307→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	var payload slackPayload
   311→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   312→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Should use attachments for changelog (auto-collapse with "Show more")
   316→	if len(payload.Attachments) != 1 {
   317→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 attachment, got %d", len(payload.Attachments))
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	att := payload.Attachments[0]
   321→
   322→	// Attachment title should show repo on provider
   323→	if !strings.Contains(att.Title, "matter-labs/zksync-era") {
   324→		t.Fatalf("expected repo in attachment title, got %q", att.Title)
   325→	}
   326→	if !strings.Contains(att.Title, "GitHub") {
   327→		t.Fatalf("expected provider label in attachment title, got %q", att.Title)
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	// Attachment text should contain changelog in a code block
   331→	if !strings.Contains(att.Text, "Fixed wrong genesis commit") {
   332→		t.Fatal("expected changelog text in attachment")
   333→	}
   334→	if !strings.Contains(att.Text, "```") {
   335→		t.Fatal("changelog should be wrapped in code block")
   336→	}
   337→	if strings.Contains(att.Text, "release_url") {
   338→		t.Fatal("raw JSON keys should not appear in formatted output")
   339→	}
   340→
   341→	// Title link should point to source
   342→	if att.TitleLink != "https://github.com/matter-labs/zksync-era/releases/tag/zkos-0.29.4-rc1" {
   343→		t.Fatalf("unexpected title_link: %s", att.TitleLink)
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	// Links should be in the attachment text
   347→	if !strings.Contains(att.Text, "View on GitHub") {
   348→		t.Fatal("expected 'View on GitHub' link in attachment text")
   349→	}
   350→	if !strings.Contains(att.Text, "View in Changelogue") {
   351→		t.Fatal("expected 'View in Changelogue' link in attachment text")
   352→	}
   353→
   354→	// No separate blocks needed
   355→	if len(payload.Blocks) != 0 {
   356→		t.Fatalf("expected no blocks (all in attachment), got %d", len(payload.Blocks))
   357→	}
   358→}

   360→func REDACTED(t *testing.T) {
   361→	var received []byte
   362→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   363→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
   364→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)

   366→	defer srv.Close()
   367→
   368→	sender := &SlackSender{Client: srv.Client()}
   369→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   370→		Type:   "slack",
   371→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	tests := []struct {
   375→		name string
   376→		msg  Notification
   377→	}{
   378→		{
   379→			name: "github release with metadata but no changelog",
   380→			msg: Notification{
   381→				Title:      "zkSync Era",
   382→				Body:       `{"prerelease":"false","release_url":"https://github.com/matter-labs/zksync-era/releases/tag/test-release-aba"}`,
   383→				Version:    "test-release-aba",

   386→				SourceURL:  "https://github.com/matter-labs/zksync-era/releases/tag/test-release-aba",
   387→				ReleaseURL: "https://changelogue.example.com/releases/rel-1",
   388→			},
   389→		},
   390→		{
   391→			name: "dockerhub release with empty metadata",
   392→			msg: Notification{
   393→				Title:      "external-node",
   394→				Body:       `{}`,
   395→				Version:    "v25.1.0",
   396→				Provider:   "dockerhub",
   397→				Repository: "matterlabs/external-node",
   398→				SourceURL:  "https://hub.docker.com/r/matterlabs/external-node/tags?name=v25.1.0",
   399→				ReleaseURL: "https://changelogue.example.com/releases/rel-2",
   400→			},
   401→		},
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	for _, tt := range tests {
   405→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   406→			err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, tt.msg)
   407→			if err != nil {
   408→				t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   409→			}
   410→
   411→			var payload slackPayload
   412→			if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   413→				t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   414→			}
   415→
   416→			// Should use blocks (no changelog for attachments)
   417→			if len(payload.Blocks) < 1 {
   418→				t.Fatal("expected at least 1 block")
   419→			}
   420→
   421→			// Header should include project name and version
   422→			header := payload.Blocks[0].Text.Text
   423→			if !strings.Contains(header, tt.msg.Title) {
   424→				t.Fatalf("header should contain project name %q, got %q", tt.msg.Title, header)
   425→			}
   426→			if !strings.Contains(header, tt.msg.Version) {
   427→				t.Fatalf("header should contain version %q, got %q", tt.msg.Version, header)
   428→			}
   429→
   430→			// Should NOT contain raw JSON in any block
   431→			for _, b := range payload.Blocks {
   432→				if b.Text != nil {
   433→					if strings.Contains(b.Text.Text, "prerelease") {
   434→						t.Fatalf("should not contain raw JSON key 'prerelease' in block text: %q", b.Text.Text)
   435→					}
   436→					if b.Text.Text == tt.msg.Body {
   437→						t.Fatalf("should not dump raw body JSON in block: %q", b.Text.Text)
   438→					}
   439→				}
   440→			}
   441→
   442→			// Should have context block with links
   443→			lastBlock := payload.Blocks[len(payload.Blocks)-1]
   444→			if lastBlock.Type != "context" {
   445→				t.Fatalf("expected last block to be context with links, got %s", lastBlock.Type)
   446→			}
   447→		})
   448→	}
   449→}

   484→			excludes: []string{"[the docs]"},
   485→		},
   486→		{
   487→			name:     "bold stripped",
   488→			input:    "This is **important** text",
   489→			contains: []string{"important"},
   490→			excludes: []string{"**"},
   491→		},
   492→		{

   496→			excludes: []string{"`"},
   497→		},

   501→			contains: []string{"screenshot"},

   503→		},
   504→		{

   507→			contains: []string{"Text", "more", "bold", "text"},
   508→			excludes: []string{"<br/>", "<b>", "</b>"},
   509→		},
   510→		{

   513→			contains: []string{

   516→				"memory leak",
   517→				"worker",
   518→			},
   519→			excludes: []string{"##", "**", "`worker`", "[v0.9...v1.0]"},
   520→		},
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	for _, tt := range tests {

   529→				}
   530→			}
   531→			for _, exclude := range tt.excludes {
   532→				if strings.Contains(result, exclude) {
   533→					t.Errorf("expected result NOT to contain %q, got:\n%s", exclude, result)
   534→				}
   535→			}
   536→		})
   537→	}
   538→}

> AGENT

Good, I have a thorough understanding of the patterns. Here's the design, section by section.

## Design: Email Notification Channel

### Architecture

The email sender follows the exact same plugin pattern as Slack/Discord:

- **New file:** `internal/routing/email.go` — `EmailSender` struct implementing `Sender` interface
- **New file:** `internal/routing/email.html.tmpl` — embedded HTML template for email body
- **New file:** `internal/routing/email_test.go` — unit tests mirroring `slack_test.go` pattern
- **One-line change:** `worker.go:NewSenders()` — add `"email": &EmailSender{}`

No database migration needed — the existing `notification_channels` table already supports arbitrary types via the `type` column and JSONB `config`.

### Config Schema

```go
type emailConfig struct {
    SMTPHost    string   `json:"smtp_host"`
    SMTPPort    int      `json:"smtp_port"`
    Username    string   `json:"username"`
    Password    string   `json:"password"`
    FromAddress string   `json:"from_address"`
    ToAddresses []string `json:"to_addresses"`
}
```

### Email Content

Two message types, matching Slack/Discord:

1. **Semantic report** — parsed from `SemanticReport` JSON body. HTML email with urgency badge, changelog summary, download command, and links.
2. **Source release fallback** — raw release with optional changelog. Simpler layout with version, changelog (if present), and links.

Both types produce **multipart MIME** (HTML + plain text). Plain text uses the existing `markdownToASCII()` helper.

### SMTP Delivery

- Go stdlib `net/smtp` with STARTTLS on port 587 (or direct TLS on 465)
- `PLAIN` auth with username/password
- 10-second timeout via context (consistent with other senders)
- Errors returned to River for retry

### HTML Template

Embedded via `//go:embed email.html.tmpl`. Clean, responsive design using inline CSS (email client compatibility). Renders urgency badges, changelog summary, download commands, and footer links in a structured layout.

Does this design look right so far?

> DEVELOPER

yes

{
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/docs/plans/2026-03-05-email-support-design.md",
  "content": "# Email Notification Channel Design\n\n## Summary\n\nAdd email as a fourth notification channel (alongside Slack, Discord, Webhook) using Go's `net/smtp` with STARTTLS and an embedded HTML template.\n\n## Architecture\n\nFollows the existing sender plugin pattern exactly:\n\n- **New file:** `internal/routing/email.go` — `EmailSender` implementing `Sender` interface\n- **New file:** `internal/routing/email.html.tmpl` — embedded HTML template\n- **New file:** `internal/routing/email_test.go` — unit tests\n- **One-line change:** `worker.go:NewSenders()` — register `\"email\"` sender\n\nNo database migration needed — `notification_channels.type` is a free-form string and `config` is JSONB.\n\n## Config Schema\n\n```go\ntype emailConfig struct {\n    SMTPHost    string   `json:\"smtp_host\"`\n    SMTPPort    int      `json:\"smtp_port\"`\n    Username    string   `json:\"username\"`\n    Password    string   `json:\"password\"`\n    FromAddress string   `json:\"from_address\"`\n    ToAddresses []string `json:\"to_addresses\"`\n}\n```\n\n## Email Content\n\nTwo message types (matching Slack/Discord pattern):\n\n1. **Semantic report** — parsed from `SemanticReport` JSON. HTML with urgency badge, changelog summary, download command, links.\n2. **Source release fallback** — raw release data. Version announcement with optional changelog and links.\n\nBoth produce multipart MIME (HTML + plain text). Plain text uses existing `markdownToASCII()`.\n\n## SMTP Delivery\n\n- Go `net/smtp` with STARTTLS (port 587) or direct TLS (port 465)\n- `PLAIN` auth with username/password\n- 10-second timeout via context\n- Errors returned to River for retry\n\n## HTML Template\n\nEmbedded via `//go:embed email.html.tmpl`. Inline CSS for email client compatibility. Responsive, clean layout with urgency color coding.\n\n## What This Does NOT Include\n\n- No per-user email preferences (uses channel-level `to_addresses`)\n- No unsubscribe links (channels are managed via API)\n- No attachment support\n- No unauthenticated SMTP relay mode\n"
}

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    11→)
    12→
    13→type webhookConfig struct {
    14→	URL string `json:"url"`
    15→}
    16→
    17→// WebhookSender sends notifications as JSON POST requests to a configured URL.
    18→type WebhookSender struct {
    19→	Client *http.Client
    20→}
    21→
    22→// webhookSemanticPayload is the structured webhook payload for semantic reports.
    23→type webhookSemanticPayload struct {
    24→	Title       string                `json:"title"`
    25→	Version     string                `json:"version"`
    26→	ProjectName string                `json:"project_name,omitempty"`
    27→	Provider    string                `json:"provider,omitempty"`
    28→	Repository  string                `json:"repository,omitempty"`
    29→	ReleaseURL  string                `json:"release_url,omitempty"`
    30→	SourceURL   string                `json:"source_url,omitempty"`
    31→	Report      *models.SemanticReport `json:"report"`
    32→}
    33→
    34→func (s *WebhookSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
    35→	var cfg webhookConfig
    36→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
    37→		return fmt.Errorf("parse webhook config: %w", err)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	// Try to parse body as SemanticReport for structured output.
    41→	var payload any
    42→	var report models.SemanticReport
    43→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
    44→		payload = webhookSemanticPayload{
    45→			Title:       msg.Title,
    46→			Version:     msg.Version,
    47→			ProjectName: msg.ProjectName,
    48→			Provider:    msg.Provider,
    49→			Repository:  msg.Repository,
    50→			ReleaseURL:  msg.ReleaseURL,
    51→			SourceURL:   msg.SourceURL,
    52→			Report:      &report,
    53→		}
    54→	} else {
    55→		payload = msg
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal notification: %w", err)
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.URL, bytes.NewReader(body))
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
    66→	}
    67→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    68→
    69→	client := s.Client
    70→	if client == nil {
    71→		client = http.DefaultClient
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
    75→	if err != nil {
    76→		return fmt.Errorf("send webhook: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→	defer resp.Body.Close()
    79→
    80→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
    81→		return fmt.Errorf("webhook returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
    82→	}
    83→	return nil
    84→}
    85→

     1→# Email Notification Channel Design
     2→
     3→## Summary
     4→
     5→Add email as a fourth notification channel (alongside Slack, Discord, Webhook) using Go's `net/smtp` with STARTTLS and an embedded HTML template.
     6→
     7→## Architecture
     8→
     9→Follows the existing sender plugin pattern exactly:
    10→
    11→- **New file:** `internal/routing/email.go` — `EmailSender` implementing `Sender` interface
    12→- **New file:** `internal/routing/email.html.tmpl` — embedded HTML template
    13→- **New file:** `internal/routing/email_test.go` — unit tests
    14→- **One-line change:** `worker.go:NewSenders()` — register `"email"` sender
    15→
    16→No database migration needed — `notification_channels.type` is a free-form string and `config` is JSONB.
    17→
    18→## Config Schema
    19→
    20→```go
    21→type emailConfig struct {
    22→    SMTPHost    string   `json:"smtp_host"`
    23→    SMTPPort    int      `json:"smtp_port"`
    24→    Username    string   `json:"username"`
    25→    Password    string   `json:"password"`
    26→    FromAddress string   `json:"from_address"`
    27→    ToAddresses []string `json:"to_addresses"`
    28→}
    29→```
    30→
    31→## Email Content
    32→
    33→Two message types (matching Slack/Discord pattern):
    34→
    35→1. **Semantic report** — parsed from `SemanticReport` JSON. HTML with urgency badge, changelog summary, download command, links.
    36→2. **Source release fallback** — raw release data. Version announcement with optional changelog and links.
    37→
    38→Both produce multipart MIME (HTML + plain text). Plain text uses existing `markdownToASCII()`.
    39→
    40→## SMTP Delivery
    41→
    42→- Go `net/smtp` with STARTTLS (port 587) or direct TLS (port 465)
    43→- `PLAIN` auth with username/password
    44→- 10-second timeout via context
    45→- Errors returned to River for retry
    46→
    47→## HTML Template
    48→
    49→Embedded via `//go:embed email.html.tmpl`. Inline CSS for email client compatibility. Responsive, clean layout with urgency color coding.
    50→
    51→## What This Does NOT Include
    52→
    53→- No per-user email preferences (uses channel-level `to_addresses`)
    54→- No unsubscribe links (channels are managed via API)
    55→- No attachment support
    56→- No unauthenticated SMTP relay mode
    57→

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"net/http"
     6→	"strings"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
     9→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/routing"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// ChannelsStore defines the persistence operations for notification channels.
    13→type ChannelsStore interface {
    14→	ListChannels(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.NotificationChannel, int, error)
    15→	CreateChannel(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel) error
    16→	GetChannel(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.NotificationChannel, error)
    17→	UpdateChannel(ctx context.Context, id string, ch *models.NotificationChannel) error
    18→	DeleteChannel(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    19→}
    20→
    21→// ChannelsHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /channels resource.
    22→type ChannelsHandler struct {
    23→	store   ChannelsStore
    24→	senders map[string]routing.Sender
    25→}
    26→
    27→// NewChannelsHandler returns a new ChannelsHandler.
    28→func NewChannelsHandler(store ChannelsStore, senders map[string]routing.Sender) *ChannelsHandler {
    29→	return &ChannelsHandler{store: store, senders: senders}
    30→}
    31→
    32→// List handles GET /channels — returns a paginated list of notification channels.
    33→func (h *ChannelsHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    34→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    35→	channels, total, err := h.store.ListChannels(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list channels")
    38→		return
    39→	}
    40→	if channels == nil {
    41→		channels = []models.NotificationChannel{}
    42→	}
    43→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, channels, page, perPage, total)
    44→}
    45→
    46→// Create handles POST /channels — creates a new notification channel.
    47→func (h *ChannelsHandler) Create(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    48→	var ch models.NotificationChannel
    49→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &ch); err != nil {
    50→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
    51→		return
    52→	}
    53→	ch.Type = strings.TrimSpace(ch.Type)
    54→	ch.Name = strings.TrimSpace(ch.Name)
    55→	if ch.Type == "" {
    56→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "type is required")
    57→		return
    58→	}
    59→	if ch.Name == "" {
    60→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "name is required")
    61→		return
    62→	}
    63→	if ch.Config == nil || string(ch.Config) == "null" {
    64→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "config is required")
    65→		return
    66→	}
    67→	if err := h.store.CreateChannel(r.Context(), &ch); err != nil {
    68→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to create channel")
    69→		return
    70→	}
    71→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, ch)
    72→}
    73→
    74→// Get handles GET /channels/{id} — returns a single notification channel.
    75→func (h *ChannelsHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    76→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    77→	if id == "" {
    78→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid channel ID")
    79→		return
    80→	}
    81→	ch, err := h.store.GetChannel(r.Context(), id)
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Channel not found")
    84→		return
    85→	}
    86→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, ch)
    87→}
    88→
    89→// Update handles PUT /channels/{id} — updates an existing notification channel.
    90→func (h *ChannelsHandler) Update(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    91→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    92→	if id == "" {
    93→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid channel ID")
    94→		return
    95→	}
    96→	var ch models.NotificationChannel
    97→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &ch); err != nil {
    98→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
    99→		return
   100→	}
   101→	ch.Type = strings.TrimSpace(ch.Type)
   102→	ch.Name = strings.TrimSpace(ch.Name)
   103→	if ch.Type == "" {
   104→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "type is required")
   105→		return
   106→	}
   107→	if ch.Name == "" {
   108→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "name is required")
   109→		return
   110→	}
   111→	if ch.Config == nil || string(ch.Config) == "null" {
   112→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "config is required")
   113→		return
   114→	}
   115→	if err := h.store.UpdateChannel(r.Context(), id, &ch); err != nil {
   116→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Channel not found")
   117→		return
   118→	}
   119→	ch.ID = id
   120→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, ch)
   121→}
   122→
   123→// Delete handles DELETE /channels/{id} — deletes a notification channel.
   124→func (h *ChannelsHandler) Delete(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   125→	id := r.PathValue("id")
   126→	if id == "" {
   127→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid channel ID")
   128→		return
   129→	}
   130→	if err := h.store.DeleteChannel(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
   131→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Channel not found")
   132→		return
   133→	}
   134→	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
   135→}
   136→
   137→// Test handles POST /channels/{id}/test — sends a test notification to verify the channel works.
   138→func (h *ChannelsHandler) Test(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   139→	id := r.PathValue("id")
   140→	if id == "" {
   141→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid channel ID")
   142→		return
   143→	}
   144→	ch, err := h.store.GetChannel(r.Context(), id)
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Channel not found")
   147→		return
   148→	}
   149→	sender, ok := h.senders[ch.Type]
   150→	if !ok {
   151→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "unsupported_type", "Unsupported channel type: "+ch.Type)
   152→		return
   153→	}
   154→	msg := routing.Notification{
   155→		Title:       "Changelogue",
   156→		Body:        `{"changelog":"## What's Changed\n\n* feat: add new monitoring dashboard by @dev in #42\n* fix: resolve memory leak in worker pool by @dev in #43\n* docs: update API reference for v1.0 by @dev in #44\n\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/example/project/compare/v0.9.0...v1.0.0","prerelease":"false"}`,
   157→		Version:     "v1.0.0-test",
   158→		ProjectName: "Test Project",
   159→		Provider:    "github",
   160→		Repository:  "example/project",
   161→		SourceURL:   "https://github.com/example/project/releases/tag/v1.0.0-test",
   162→		ReleaseURL:  "https://changelogue.example.com/releases/test",
   163→	}
   164→	if err := sender.Send(r.Context(), ch, msg); err != nil {
   165→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, "send_failed", "Failed to send test notification: "+err.Error())
   166→		return
   167→	}
   168→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"status": "ok"})
   169→}
   170→
