`internal/routing/email.go`:\n\n```go\npackage routing\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"crypto/tls\"\n\t_ \"embed\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"html/template\"\n\t\"mime/multipart\"\n\t\"net\"\n\t\"net/smtp\"\n\t\"net/textproto\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n)\n\n//go:embed email.html.tmpl\nvar emailTemplateRaw string\n\nvar emailTmpl = template.Must(template.New(\"email\").Parse(emailTemplateRaw))\n\ntype emailConfig struct {\n\tSMTPHost    string   `json:\"smtp_host\"`\n\tSMTPPort    int      `json:\"smtp_port\"`\n\tUsername    string   `json:\"username\"`\n\tPassword    string   `json:\"password\"`\n\tFromAddress string   `json:\"from_address\"`\n\tToAddresses []string `json:\"to_addresses\"`\n}\n\n// emailData is the template rendering context.\ntype emailData struct {\n\tSubject         string\n\tUrgencyBadge    string\n\tUrgencyColor    string\n\tUrgencyReason   string\n\tSummary         string\n\tChangelog       string\n\tDownloadCommand string\n\tSourceURL       string\n\tReleaseURL      string\n\tProviderLabel   string\n\tFooterText      string\n}\n\n// EmailSender sends notifications via SMTP email.\ntype EmailSender struct{}\n\nfunc urgencyColor(level string) string {\n\tswitch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {\n\tcase \"CRITICAL\":\n\t\treturn \"#111113\"\n\tcase \"HIGH\":\n\t\treturn \"#DC2626\"\n\tcase \"MEDIUM\":\n\t\treturn \"#D97706\"\n\tcase \"LOW\":\n\t\treturn \"#16A34A\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"#2563EB\"\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (s *EmailSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {\n\tvar cfg emailConfig\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse email config: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdata := s.buildEmailData(msg)\n\n\t// Render HTML body.\n\tvar htmlBuf bytes.Buffer\n\tif err := emailTmpl.Execute(&htmlBuf, data); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"render email template: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build plain text body.\n\tplainText := s.buildPlainText(data)\n\n\t// Assemble multipart/alternative MIME message.\n\tvar msgBuf bytes.Buffer\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"From: %s\\r\\n\", cfg.FromAddress))\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"To: %s\\r\\n\", strings.Join(cfg.ToAddresses, \", \")))\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"Subject: %s\\r\\n\", data.Subject))\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(\"MIME-Version: 1.0\\r\\n\")\n\n\tmw := multipart.NewWriter(&msgBuf)\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"Content-Type: multipart/alternative; boundary=%s\\r\\n\\r\\n\", mw.Boundary()))\n\n\t// Plain text part.\n\ttextHeader := make(textproto.MIMEHeader)\n\ttextHeader.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/plain; charset=utf-8\")\n\tpw, err := mw.CreatePart(textHeader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create text part: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tpw.Write([]byte(plainText))\n\n\t// HTML part.\n\thtmlHeader := make(textproto.MIMEHeader)\n\thtmlHeader.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/html; charset=utf-8\")\n\thw, err := mw.CreatePart(htmlHeader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create html part: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\thw.Write(htmlBuf.Bytes())\n\n\tmw.Close()\n\n\t// Send via SMTP.\n\taddr := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s:%d\", cfg.SMTPHost, cfg.SMTPPort)\n\n\tvar client *smtp.Client\n\tif cfg.SMTPPort == 465 {\n\t\t// Direct TLS (port 465).\n\t\tconn, err := tls.DialWithDialer(\n\t\t\t&net.Dialer{},\n\t\t\t\"tcp\", addr,\n\t\t\t&tls.Config{ServerName: cfg.SMTPHost},\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"tls dial: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tclient, err = smtp.NewClient(conn, cfg.SMTPHost)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tconn.Close()\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp client over tls: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// STARTTLS (port 587 or other).\n\t\tclient, err = smtp.Dial(addr)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp dial: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := client.Hello(\"localhost\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\tclient.Close()\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp hello: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Only attempt STARTTLS if the server supports it.\n\t\tif ok, _ := client.Extension(\"STARTTLS\"); ok {\n\t\t\tif err := client.StartTLS(&tls.Config{ServerName: cfg.SMTPHost}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tclient.Close()\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp starttls: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tdefer client.Close()\n\n\tauth := smtp.PlainAuth(\"\", cfg.Username, cfg.Password, cfg.SMTPHost)\n\tif err := client.Auth(auth); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp auth: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := client.Mail(cfg.FromAddress); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp mail from: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfor _, to := range cfg.ToAddresses {\n\t\tif err := client.Rcpt(to); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp rcpt to %s: %w\", to, err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\twc, err := client.Data()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp data: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := wc.Write(msgBuf.Bytes()); err != nil {\n\t\twc.Close()\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp write: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := wc.Close(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp data close: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn client.Quit()\n}\n\nfunc (s *EmailSender) buildEmailData(msg Notification) emailData {\n\tdata := emailData{\n\t\tSourceURL:     msg.SourceURL,\n\t\tReleaseURL:    msg.ReleaseURL,\n\t\tProviderLabel: ProviderLabel(msg.Provider),\n\t\tUrgencyColor:  \"#2563EB\", // default blue\n\t}\n\n\t// Footer\n\tif msg.Provider != \"\" && msg.Repository != \"\" {\n\t\tdata.FooterText = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s · %s\", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)\n\t}\n\n\t// Try semantic report first.\n\tvar report models.SemanticReport\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != \"\" {\n\t\turgency := report.Urgency\n\t\tif urgency == \"\" {\n\t\t\turgency = report.RiskLevel\n\t\t}\n\t\turgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason\n\t\tif urgencyReason == \"\" {\n\t\t\turgencyReason = report.RiskReason\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tdata.Subject = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", msg.Title, msg.Version)\n\t\tdata.UrgencyColor = urgencyColor(urgency)\n\t\tif urgency != \"\" {\n\t\t\tdata.UrgencyBadge = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s Urgency\", urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)\n\t\t}\n\t\tupperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))\n\t\tif (upperUrgency == \"CRITICAL\" || upperUrgency == \"HIGH\") && urgencyReason != \"\" {\n\t\t\tdata.UrgencyReason = urgencyReason\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummary := report.ChangelogSummary\n\t\tif summary == \"\" {\n\t\t\tsummary = report.Summary\n\t\t}\n\t\tdata.Summary = summary\n\t\tif len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {\n\t\t\tdata.DownloadCommand = report.DownloadCommands[0]\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn data\n\t}\n\n\t// Fallback: source-level release.\n\tdata.Subject = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", msg.Title, msg.Version)\n\tif fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok && fields.Changelog != \"\" {\n\t\tdata.Changelog = markdownToASCII(fields.Changelog)\n\t}\n\treturn data\n}\n\nfunc (s *EmailSender) buildPlainText(data emailData) string {\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.WriteString(data.Subject + \"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(strings.Repeat(\"=\", len(data.Subject)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\tif data.UrgencyBadge != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(data.UrgencyBadge + \"\\n\")\n\t\tif data.UrgencyReason != \"\" {\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(data.UrgencyReason + \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.Summary != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(data.Summary + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.Changelog != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(data.Changelog + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.DownloadCommand != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  \" + data.DownloadCommand + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.SourceURL != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"View on \" + data.ProviderLabel + \": \" + data.SourceURL + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\tif data.ReleaseURL != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"View in Changelogue: \" + data.ReleaseURL + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.FooterText != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n---\\n\" + data.FooterText + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run tests**\n\nRun: `go test -v -run TestEmailSender ./internal/routing/...`\nExpected: All 4 tests PASS.\n\n**Step 3:      5→**Goal:** Add email as a notification channel so users can receive release alerts via SMTP email.

     7→**Architecture:** Implements the `Sender` interface with an `EmailSender` struct. Uses Go's `net/smtp` for STARTTLS delivery and `html/template` with `//go:embed` for HTML email rendering. Multipart MIME (HTML + plain text).
     8→
     9→**Tech Stack:** Go stdlib (`net/smtp`, `html/template`, `mime/multipart`, `crypto/tls`)
    10→
    11→---

    13→### Task 1: Create HTML email template
    14→
    15→**Files:**

    18→**Step 1: Write the HTML template**
    19→
    20→Create `internal/routing/email.html.tmpl` with this content:
    21→
    22→```html
    23→<!DOCTYPE html>
    24→<html>
    25→<head>
    26→<meta charset="utf-8">
    27→<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    28→<title>{{.Subject}}</title>
    29→</head>
    30→<body style="margin:0;padding:0;background-color:#f4f4f5;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,sans-serif;">
    31→<table width="100%" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="background-color:#f4f4f5;padding:24px 0;">
    32→<tr><td align="center">
    33→<table width="600" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="background-color:#ffffff;border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;">
    34→
    35→<!-- Header -->
    36→<tr><td style="background-color:{{.UrgencyColor}};padding:20px 24px;">
    37→<span style="color:#ffffff;font-size:20px;font-weight:600;">{{.Subject}}</span>
    38→</td></tr>
    39→
    40→{{if .UrgencyBadge}}
    41→<!-- Urgency Badge -->
    42→<tr><td style="padding:16px 24px 0;">
    43→<span style="display:inline-block;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:4px;background-color:{{.UrgencyColor}};color:#ffffff;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;">{{.UrgencyBadge}}</span>
    44→{{if .UrgencyReason}}<p style="margin:8px 0 0;color:#71717a;font-size:14px;">{{.UrgencyReason}}</p>{{end}}
    45→</td></tr>
    46→{{end}}
    47→
    48→{{if .Summary}}
    49→<!-- Summary -->
    50→<tr><td style="padding:16px 24px 0;">
    51→<p style="margin:0;color:#27272a;font-size:15px;line-height:1.6;">{{.Summary}}</p>
    52→</td></tr>
    53→{{end}}
    54→
    55→{{if .Changelog}}
    56→<!-- Changelog -->
    57→<tr><td style="padding:16px 24px 0;">
    58→<div style="background-color:#f4f4f5;border-radius:6px;padding:12px 16px;">
    59→<pre style="margin:0;font-family:'SF Mono',Monaco,Consolas,monospace;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;color:#3f3f46;white-space:pre-wrap;word-wrap:break-word;">{{.Changelog}}</pre>
    60→</div>
    61→</td></tr>
    62→{{end}}
    63→
    64→{{if .DownloadCommand}}
    65→<!-- Download Command -->
    66→<tr><td style="padding:16px 24px 0;">
    67→<code style="display:block;background-color:#18181b;color:#e4e4e7;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:6px;font-family:'SF Mono',Monaco,Consolas,monospace;font-size:13px;">{{.DownloadCommand}}</code>
    68→</td></tr>
    69→{{end}}
    70→
    71→<!-- Links -->
    72→<tr><td style="padding:16px 24px;">
    73→{{if .SourceURL}}<a href="{{.SourceURL}}" style="color:#2563eb;font-size:14px;text-decoration:none;">View on {{.ProviderLabel}}</a>{{end}}
    74→{{if and .SourceURL .ReleaseURL}}<span style="color:#d4d4d8;margin:0 8px;">|</span>{{end}}
    75→{{if .ReleaseURL}}<a href="{{.ReleaseURL}}" style="color:#2563eb;font-size:14px;text-decoration:none;">View in Changelogue</a>{{end}}
    76→</td></tr>
    77→
    78→<!-- Footer -->
    79→<tr><td style="padding:12px 24px;border-top:1px solid #e4e4e7;">
    80→<p style="margin:0;color:#a1a1aa;font-size:12px;">{{.FooterText}}</p>
    81→</td></tr>
    82→
    83→</table>
    84→</td></tr>
    85→</table>
    86→</body>
    87→</html>
    88→```

   104→**Step 1: Write the test file**

   106→Create `internal/routing/email_test.go`:
   107→
   108→```go
   109→package routing
   110→
   111→import (
   112→	"context"
   113→	"encoding/json"
   114→	"net"
   115→	"strings"
   116→	"testing"
   117→
   118→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
   119→)
   120→
   121→// mockSMTPServer starts a minimal SMTP server that captures the DATA payload.
   122→// Returns the listener and a channel that receives the raw email message.
   123→func mockSMTPServer(t *testing.T) (net.Listener, <-chan string) {
   124→	t.Helper()
   125→	ln, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		t.Fatalf("listen: %v", err)
   128→	}
   129→	ch := make(chan string, 1)
   130→
   131→	go func() {
   132→		conn, err := ln.Accept()
   133→		if err != nil {
   134→			return
   135→		}
   136→		defer conn.Close()
   137→
   138→		write := func(s string) { conn.Write([]byte(s + "\r\n")) }
   139→		read := func() string {
   140→			buf := make([]byte, 4096)
   141→			n, _ := conn.Read(buf)
   142→			return string(buf[:n])
   143→		}
   144→
   145→		write("220 localhost ESMTP")
   146→		cmd := read()
   147→		if strings.HasPrefix(cmd, "EHLO") {
   148→			write("250-localhost")
   149→			write("250 AUTH PLAIN LOGIN")
   150→		}
   151→		read() // AUTH
   152→		write("235 Authentication successful")
   153→		read() // MAIL FROM
   154→		write("250 OK")
   155→		read() // RCPT TO
   156→		write("250 OK")
   157→
   158→		// Handle additional RCPT TO commands
   159→		for {
   160→			line := read()
   161→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "RCPT TO") {
   162→				write("250 OK")
   163→				continue
   164→			}
   165→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "DATA") {
   166→				write("354 Start mail input")
   167→				break
   168→			}
   169→		}
   170→
   171→		// Read DATA until lone "."
   172→		var data strings.Builder
   173→		for {
   174→			line := read()
   175→			data.WriteString(line)
   176→			if strings.Contains(line, "\r\n.\r\n") {
   177→				break
   178→			}
   179→		}
   180→		write("250 OK")
   181→		ch <- data.String()
   182→
   183→		read() // QUIT
   184→		write("221 Bye")
   185→	}()
   186→
   187→	return ln, ch
   188→}
   189→
   190→func TestEmailSender_Send(t *testing.T) {
   191→	ln, dataCh := mockSMTPServer(t)
   192→	defer ln.Close()
   193→
   194→	addr := ln.Addr().String()
   195→	host, port, _ := net.SplitHostPort(addr)
   196→
   197→	sender := &EmailSender{}
   198→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   199→		Type: "email",
   200→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{
   201→			"smtp_host": "` + host + `",
   202→			"smtp_port": ` + port + `,
   203→			"username": "user",
   204→			"password": "pass",
   205→			"from_address": "noreply@example.com",
   206→			"to_addresses": ["team@example.com"]
   207→		}`),
   208→	}
   209→	msg := Notification{
   210→		Title:      "geth",
   211→		Body:       `{"changelog":"Security fixes and performance improvements"}`,
   212→		Version:    "v1.14.0",
   213→		Provider:   "github",
   214→		Repository: "ethereum/go-ethereum",
   215→		SourceURL:  "https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.14.0",
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   219→	if err != nil {
   220→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	data := <-dataCh
   224→	if !strings.Contains(data, "Content-Type: multipart/alternative") {
   225→		t.Fatal("expected multipart/alternative content type")
   226→	}
   227→	if !strings.Contains(data, "text/plain") {
   228→		t.Fatal("expected text/plain part")
   229→	}
   230→	if !strings.Contains(data, "text/html") {
   231→		t.Fatal("expected text/html part")
   232→	}
   233→	if !strings.Contains(data, "geth v1.14.0") {
   234→		t.Fatal("expected subject to contain project name and version")
   235→	}
   236→}
   237→

   357→
   358→### Task 3: Implement EmailSender
   359→
   360→**Files:**
   361→- Create: `internal/routing/email.go`
   362→
   363→**Step 1: Write the implementation**
   364→
   365→Create `internal/routing/email.go`:
   366→
   367→```go
   368→package routing
   369→
   370→import (
   371→	"bytes"
   372→	"context"
   373→	"crypto/tls"
   374→	_ "embed"
   375→	"encoding/json"
   376→	"fmt"
   377→	"html/template"
   378→	"mime/multipart"
   379→	"net"
   380→	"net/smtp"
   381→	"net/textproto"
   382→	"strings"
   383→
   384→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
   385→)
   386→
   387→//go:embed email.html.tmpl
   388→var emailTemplateRaw string
   389→
   390→var emailTmpl = template.Must(template.New("email").Parse(emailTemplateRaw))
   391→
   392→type emailConfig struct {
   393→	SMTPHost    string   `json:"smtp_host"`
   394→	SMTPPort    int      `json:"smtp_port"`
   395→	Username    string   `json:"username"`
   396→	Password    string   `json:"password"`
   397→	FromAddress string   `json:"from_address"`
   398→	ToAddresses []string `json:"to_addresses"`
   399→}
   400→
   401→// emailData is the template rendering context.
   402→type emailData struct {
   403→	Subject         string
   404→	UrgencyBadge    string
   405→	UrgencyColor    string
   406→	UrgencyReason   string
   407→	Summary         string
   408→	Changelog       string
   409→	DownloadCommand string
   410→	SourceURL       string
   411→	ReleaseURL      string
   412→	ProviderLabel   string
   413→	FooterText      string
   414→}
   415→
   416→// EmailSender sends notifications via SMTP email.
   417→type EmailSender struct{}
   418→
   419→func urgencyColor(level string) string {
   420→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
   421→	case "CRITICAL":
   422→		return "#111113"
   423→	case "HIGH":
   424→		return "#DC2626"
   425→	case "MEDIUM":
   426→		return "#D97706"
   427→	case "LOW":
   428→		return "#16A34A"
   429→	default:
   430→		return "#2563EB"
   431→	}
   432→}
   433→
   434→func (s *EmailSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   435→	var cfg emailConfig
   436→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   437→		return fmt.Errorf("parse email config: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→
   440→	data := s.buildEmailData(msg)
   441→
   442→	// Render HTML body.
   443→	var htmlBuf bytes.Buffer
   444→	if err := emailTmpl.Execute(&htmlBuf, data); err != nil {
   445→		return fmt.Errorf("render email template: %w", err)
   446→	}
   447→
   448→	// Build plain text body.
   449→	plainText := s.buildPlainText(data)
   450→
   451→	// Assemble multipart/alternative MIME message.
   452→	var msgBuf bytes.Buffer
   453→	msgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("From: %s\r\n", cfg.FromAddress))
   454→	msgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("To: %s\r\n", strings.Join(cfg.ToAddresses, ", ")))
   455→	msgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Subject: %s\r\n", data.Subject))
   456→	msgBuf.WriteString("MIME-Version: 1.0\r\n")
   457→
   458→	mw := multipart.NewWriter(&msgBuf)
   459→	msgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Content-Type: multipart/alternative; boundary=%s\r\n\r\n", mw.Boundary()))
   460→
   461→	// Plain text part.
   462→	textHeader := make(textproto.MIMEHeader)
   463→	textHeader.Set("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
   464→	pw, err := mw.CreatePart(textHeader)
   465→	if err != nil {
   466→		return fmt.Errorf("create text part: %w", err)
   467→	}
   468→	pw.Write([]byte(plainText))
   469→
   470→	// HTML part.
   471→	htmlHeader := make(textproto.MIMEHeader)
   472→	htmlHeader.Set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
   473→	hw, err := mw.CreatePart(htmlHeader)
   474→	if err != nil {
   475→		return fmt.Errorf("create html part: %w", err)
   476→	}
   477→	hw.Write(htmlBuf.Bytes())
   478→
   479→	mw.Close()
   480→
   481→	// Send via SMTP.
   482→	addr := fmt.Sprintf("%s:%d", cfg.SMTPHost, cfg.SMTPPort)
   483→
   484→	var client *smtp.Client
   485→	if cfg.SMTPPort == 465 {
   486→		// Direct TLS (port 465).
   487→		conn, err := tls.DialWithDialer(
   488→			&net.Dialer{},
   489→			"tcp", addr,
   490→			&tls.Config{ServerName: cfg.SMTPHost},
   491→		)
   492→		if err != nil {
   493→			return fmt.Errorf("tls dial: %w", err)
   494→		}
   495→		client, err = smtp.NewClient(conn, cfg.SMTPHost)
   496→		if err != nil {
   497→			conn.Close()
   498→			return fmt.Errorf("smtp client over tls: %w", err)
   499→		}
   500→	} else {
   501→		// STARTTLS (port 587 or other).
   502→		client, err = smtp.Dial(addr)
   503→		if err != nil {
   504→			return fmt.Errorf("smtp dial: %w", err)
   505→		}
   506→		if err := client.Hello("localhost"); err != nil {
   507→			client.Close()
   508→			return fmt.Errorf("smtp hello: %w", err)
   509→		}
   510→		// Only attempt STARTTLS if the server supports it.
   511→		if ok, _ := client.Extension("STARTTLS"); ok {
   512→			if err := client.StartTLS(&tls.Config{ServerName: cfg.SMTPHost}); err != nil {
   513→				client.Close()
   514→				return fmt.Errorf("smtp starttls: %w", err)
   515→			}
   516→		}
   517→	}
   518→	defer client.Close()
   519→
   520→	auth := smtp.PlainAuth("", cfg.Username, cfg.Password, cfg.SMTPHost)
   521→	if err := client.Auth(auth); err != nil {
   522→		return fmt.Errorf("smtp auth: %w", err)
   523→	}
   524→
   525→	if err := client.Mail(cfg.FromAddress); err != nil {
   526→		return fmt.Errorf("smtp mail from: %w", err)
   527→	}
   528→	for _, to := range cfg.ToAddresses {
   529→		if err := client.Rcpt(to); err != nil {
   530→			return fmt.Errorf("smtp rcpt to %s: %w", to, err)
   531→		}
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	wc, err := client.Data()
   535→	if err != nil {
   536→		return fmt.Errorf("smtp data: %w", err)
   537→	}
   538→	if _, err := wc.Write(msgBuf.Bytes()); err != nil {
   539→		wc.Close()
   540→		return fmt.Errorf("smtp write: %w", err)
   541→	}
   542→	if err := wc.Close(); err != nil {
   543→		return fmt.Errorf("smtp data close: %w", err)
   544→	}
   545→
   546→	return client.Quit()
   547→}
   548→
   549→func (s *EmailSender) buildEmailData(msg Notification) emailData {
   550→	data := emailData{
   551→		SourceURL:     msg.SourceURL,
   552→		ReleaseURL:    msg.ReleaseURL,
   553→		ProviderLabel: ProviderLabel(msg.Provider),
   554→		UrgencyColor:  "#2563EB", // default blue
   555→	}
   556→
   557→	// Footer
   558→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   559→		data.FooterText = fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	// Try semantic report first.
   563→	var report models.SemanticReport
   564→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   565→		urgency := report.Urgency
   566→		if urgency == "" {
   567→			urgency = report.RiskLevel
   568→		}
   569→		urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
   570→		if urgencyReason == "" {
   571→			urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
   572→		}
   573→
   574→		data.Subject = fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", msg.Title, msg.Version)
   575→		data.UrgencyColor = urgencyColor(urgency)
   576→		if urgency != "" {
   577→			data.UrgencyBadge = fmt.Sprintf("%s %s Urgency", urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)
   578→		}
   579→		upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
   580→		if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
   581→			data.UrgencyReason = urgencyReason
   582→		}
   583→		summary := report.ChangelogSummary
   584→		if summary == "" {
   585→			summary = report.Summary
   586→		}
   587→		data.Summary = summary
   588→		if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   589→			data.DownloadCommand = report.DownloadCommands[0]
   590→		}
   591→		return data
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	// Fallback: source-level release.
   595→	data.Subject = fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", msg.Title, msg.Version)
   596→	if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok && fields.Changelog != "" {
   597→		data.Changelog = markdownToASCII(fields.Changelog)
   598→	}
   599→	return data
   600→}
   601→
   602→func (s *EmailSender) buildPlainText(data emailData) string {
   603→	var b strings.Builder
   604→	b.WriteString(data.Subject + "\n")
   605→	b.WriteString(strings.Repeat("=", len(data.Subject)) + "\n\n")
   606→
   607→	if data.UrgencyBadge != "" {
   608→		b.WriteString(data.UrgencyBadge + "\n")
   609→		if data.UrgencyReason != "" {
   610→			b.WriteString(data.UrgencyReason + "\n")
   611→		}
   612→		b.WriteString("\n")
   613→	}
   614→
   615→	if data.Summary != "" {
   616→		b.WriteString(data.Summary + "\n\n")
   617→	}
   618→
   619→	if data.Changelog != "" {
   620→		b.WriteString(data.Changelog + "\n\n")
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	if data.DownloadCommand != "" {
   624→		b.WriteString("  " + data.DownloadCommand + "\n\n")
   625→	}
   626→
   627→	if data.SourceURL != "" {
   628→		b.WriteString("View on " + data.ProviderLabel + ": " + data.SourceURL + "\n")
   629→	}
   630→	if data.ReleaseURL != "" {
   631→		b.WriteString("View in Changelogue: " + data.ReleaseURL + "\n")
   632→	}
   633→
   634→	if data.FooterText != "" {
   635→		b.WriteString("\n---\n" + data.FooterText + "\n")
   636→	}
   637→
   638→	return b.String()
   639→}
   640→```
   641→
   642→**Step 2: Run tests**
   643→
   644→Run: `go test -v -run TestEmailSender ./internal/routing/...`
   645→Expected: All 4 tests PASS.

\"embed\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"html/template\"\n\t\"mime/multipart\"\n\t\"net\"\n\t\"net/smtp\"\n\t\"net/textproto\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n)\n\n//go:embed email.html.tmpl\nvar emailTemplateRaw string\n\nvar emailTmpl = template.Must(template.New(\"email\").Parse(emailTemplateRaw))\n\ntype emailConfig struct {\n\tSMTPHost    string   `json:\"smtp_host\"`\n\tSMTPPort    int      `json:\"smtp_port\"`\n\tUsername    string   `json:\"username\"`\n\tPassword    string   `json:\"password\"`\n\tFromAddress string   `json:\"from_address\"`\n\tToAddresses []string `json:\"to_addresses\"`\n}\n\n// emailData is the template rendering context.\ntype emailData struct {\n\tSubject         string\n\tUrgencyBadge    string\n\tUrgencyColor    string\n\tUrgencyReason   string\n\tSummary         string\n\tChangelog       string\n\tDownloadCommand string\n\tSourceURL       string\n\tReleaseURL      string\n\tProviderLabel   string\n\tFooterText      string\n}\n\n// EmailSender sends notifications via SMTP email.\ntype EmailSender struct{}\n\nfunc urgencyColor(level string) string {\n\tswitch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {\n\tcase \"CRITICAL\":\n\t\treturn \"#111113\"\n\tcase \"HIGH\":\n\t\treturn \"#DC2626\"\n\tcase \"MEDIUM\":\n\t\treturn \"#D97706\"\n\tcase \"LOW\":\n\t\treturn \"#16A34A\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"#2563EB\"\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (s *EmailSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {\n\tvar cfg emailConfig\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse email config: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdata := s.buildEmailData(msg)\n\n\t// Render HTML body.\n\tvar htmlBuf bytes.Buffer\n\tif err := emailTmpl.Execute(&htmlBuf, data); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"render email template: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build plain text body.\n\tplainText := s.buildPlainText(data)\n\n\t// Assemble multipart/alternative MIME message.\n\tvar msgBuf bytes.Buffer\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"From: %s\\r\\n\", cfg.FromAddress))\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"To: %s\\r\\n\", strings.Join(cfg.ToAddresses, \", \")))\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"Subject: %s\\r\\n\", data.Subject))\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(\"MIME-Version: 1.0\\r\\n\")\n\n\tmw := multipart.NewWriter(&msgBuf)\n\tmsgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"Content-Type: multipart/alternative; boundary=%s\\r\\n\\r\\n\", mw.Boundary()))\n\n\t// Plain text part.\n\ttextHeader := make(textproto.MIMEHeader)\n\ttextHeader.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/plain; charset=utf-8\")\n\tpw, err := mw.CreatePart(textHeader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create text part: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tpw.Write([]byte(plainText))\n\n\t// HTML part.\n\thtmlHeader := make(textproto.MIMEHeader)\n\thtmlHeader.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/html; charset=utf-8\")\n\thw, err := mw.CreatePart(htmlHeader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create html part: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\thw.Write(htmlBuf.Bytes())\n\n\tmw.Close()\n\n\t// Send via SMTP.\n\taddr := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s:%d\", cfg.SMTPHost, cfg.SMTPPort)\n\n\tvar client *smtp.Client\n\tif cfg.SMTPPort == 465 {\n\t\t// Direct TLS (port 465).\n\t\tconn, err := tls.DialWithDialer(\n\t\t\t&net.Dialer{},\n\t\t\t\"tcp\", addr,\n\t\t\t&tls.Config{ServerName: cfg.SMTPHost},\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"tls dial: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tclient, err = smtp.NewClient(conn, cfg.SMTPHost)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tconn.Close()\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp client over tls: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// STARTTLS (port 587 or other).\n\t\tclient, err = smtp.Dial(addr)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp dial: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := client.Hello(\"localhost\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\tclient.Close()\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp hello: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Only attempt STARTTLS if the server supports it.\n\t\tif ok, _ := client.Extension(\"STARTTLS\"); ok {\n\t\t\tif err := client.StartTLS(&tls.Config{ServerName: cfg.SMTPHost}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tclient.Close()\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp starttls: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tdefer client.Close()\n\n\tauth := smtp.PlainAuth(\"\", cfg.Username, cfg.Password, cfg.SMTPHost)\n\tif err := client.Auth(auth); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp auth: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := client.Mail(cfg.FromAddress); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp mail from: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfor _, to := range cfg.ToAddresses {\n\t\tif err := client.Rcpt(to); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp rcpt to %s: %w\", to, err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\twc, err := client.Data()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp data: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := wc.Write(msgBuf.Bytes()); err != nil {\n\t\twc.Close()\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp write: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := wc.Close(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp data close: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn client.Quit()\n}\n\nfunc (s *EmailSender) buildEmailData(msg Notification) emailData {\n\tdata := emailData{\n\t\tSourceURL:     msg.SourceURL,\n\t\tReleaseURL:    msg.ReleaseURL,\n\t\tProviderLabel: ProviderLabel(msg.Provider),\n\t\tUrgencyColor:  \"#2563EB\", // default blue\n\t}\n\n\t// Footer\n\tif msg.Provider != \"\" && msg.Repository != \"\" {\n\t\tdata.FooterText = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s · %s\", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)\n\t}\n\n\t// Try semantic report first.\n\tvar report models.SemanticReport\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != \"\" {\n\t\turgency := report.Urgency\n\t\tif urgency == \"\" {\n\t\t\turgency = report.RiskLevel\n\t\t}\n\t\turgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason\n\t\tif urgencyReason == \"\" {\n\t\t\turgencyReason = report.RiskReason\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tdata.Subject = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", msg.Title, msg.Version)\n\t\tdata.UrgencyColor = urgencyColor(urgency)\n\t\tif urgency != \"\" {\n\t\t\tdata.UrgencyBadge = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s Urgency\", urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)\n\t\t}\n\t\tupperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))\n\t\tif (upperUrgency == \"CRITICAL\" || upperUrgency == \"HIGH\") && urgencyReason != \"\" {\n\t\t\tdata.UrgencyReason = urgencyReason\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummary := report.ChangelogSummary\n\t\tif summary == \"\" {\n\t\t\tsummary = report.Summary\n\t\t}\n\t\tdata.Summary = summary\n\t\tif len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {\n\t\t\tdata.DownloadCommand = report.DownloadCommands[0]\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn data\n\t}\n\n\t// Fallback: source-level release.\n\tdata.Subject = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", msg.Title, msg.Version)\n\tif fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok && fields.Changelog != \"\" {\n\t\tdata.Changelog = markdownToASCII(fields.Changelog)\n\t}\n\treturn data\n}\n\nfunc (s *EmailSender) buildPlainText(data emailData) string {\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.WriteString(data.Subject + \"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(strings.Repeat(\"=\", len(data.Subject)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\tif data.UrgencyBadge != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(data.UrgencyBadge + \"\\n\")\n\t\tif data.UrgencyReason != \"\" {\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(data.UrgencyReason + \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.Summary != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(data.Summary + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.Changelog != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(data.Changelog + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.DownloadCommand != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  \" + data.DownloadCommand + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.SourceURL != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"View on \" + data.ProviderLabel + \": \" + data.SourceURL + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\tif data.ReleaseURL != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"View in Changelogue: \" + data.ReleaseURL + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tif data.FooterText != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n---\\n\" + data.FooterText + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n```\n\n## File 3: internal/routing/email_test.go\n\nCreate this file with EXACTLY this content:\n\n```go\npackage routing\n\nimport captures the DATA payload.\n// Returns the listener and a channel that receives the raw email message.\nfunc mockSMTPServer(t *testing.T) (net.Listener, <-chan string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tln, err := net.Listen(\"tcp\", \"127.0.0.1:0\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"listen: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tch := make(chan string, 1)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tconn, err := ln.Accept()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tdefer conn.Close()\n\n\t\twrite := func(s string) { conn.Write([]byte(s + \"\\r\\n\")) }\n\t\tread := func() string {\n\t\t\tbuf := make([]byte, 4096)\n\t\t\tn, _ := conn.Read(buf)\n\t\t\treturn string(buf[:n])\n\t\t}\n\n\t\twrite(\"220 localhost ESMTP\")\n\t\tcmd := read()\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(cmd, \"EHLO\") {\n\t\t\twrite(\"250-localhost\")\n\t\t\twrite(\"250 AUTH PLAIN LOGIN\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tread() // AUTH\n\t\twrite(\"235 Authentication successful\")\n\t\tread() // MAIL FROM\n\t\twrite(\"250 OK\")\n\t\tread() // RCPT TO\n\t\twrite(\"250 OK\")\n\n\t\t// Handle additional RCPT TO commands\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\tline := read()\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(line, \"RCPT TO\") {\n\t\t\t\twrite(\"250 OK\")\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(line, \"DATA\") {\n\t\t\t\twrite(\"354 Start mail input\")\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Read DATA until lone \".\"\n\t\tvar data strings.Builder\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\tline := read()\n\t\t\tdata.WriteString(line)\n\t\t\tif strings.Contains(line, \"\\r\\n.\\r\\n\") {\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\twrite(\"250 OK\")\n\t\tch <- data.String()\n\n\t\tread() // QUIT\n\t\twrite(\"221 Bye\")\n\t}()\n\n\treturn ln, ch\n}\n\nfunc TestEmailSender_Send(t *testing.T) {\n\tln, dataCh := mockSMTPServer(t)\n\tdefer ## Files Created

1. **`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.html.tmpl`** -- HTML email template with responsive table-based layout, urgency-colored header, badge, summary, changelog, download command, links, and footer sections.

2. **`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.go`** -- `EmailSender` implementation that:
   - Parses `emailConfig` from the channel's JSONB config (SMTP host/port, credentials, from/to addresses)
   - Builds rich email data from `SemanticReport` JSON (urgency badge, summary, download commands) with fallback to raw release body
   - Renders both HTML (via the embedded template) and plain text bodies
   - Assembles a `multipart/alternative` MIME message
   - Sends via SMTP with support for both direct TLS (port 465) and STARTTLS (port 587+)

3. **`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email_test.go`** -- Four tests using a mock SMTP server:
   - `TestEmailSender_Send` -- basic send with changelog fallback
   - `TestEmailSender_SemanticReport` -- rich formatting from semantic report JSON
   - `TestEmailSender_InvalidConfig` -- error handling for malformed config
   - `TestEmailSender_MultipleRecipients` -- verifies multiple recipients in To header

    29→// NotifyWorker is a River worker that processes NotifyJobArgs.

All tests pass. I now have a complete picture. Here is my review.

---

# Email Notification Channel -- Code Review

## What Was Done Well

- The implementation follows the existing `Sender` interface pattern exactly as the Slack and Discord senders do: config struct unmarshalled from JSONB, semantic report parsing with fallback, shared helpers (`urgencyEmoji`, `ProviderLabel`, `parseRawBody`, `markdownToASCII`).
- The file set matches the design spec precisely: `email.go`, `email.html.tmpl`, `email_test.go`, and a one-line registration in `worker.go`.
- The HTML template uses table-based layout with inline CSS, which is the correct approach for email client compatibility.
- Multipart MIME with plain text + HTML alternative is correctly structured.
- The STARTTLS vs direct TLS (port 465) branching is appropriate and matches the design spec.
- All four tests pass cleanly. The mock SMTP server is a solid approach for unit testing without external dependencies.

---

## Critical Issues (Must Fix)

### 1. SMTP header injection via unsanitized `FromAddress` and `ToAddresses`

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.go`, lines 86-88

```go
msgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("From: %s\r\n", cfg.FromAddress))
msgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("To: %s\r\n", strings.Join(cfg.ToAddresses, ", ")))
msgBuf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Subject: %s\r\n", data.Subject))
```

The `FromAddress`, `ToAddresses`, and `Subject` values are written directly into MIME headers with no sanitization. If any of these contain `\r\n`, an attacker who controls the channel config could inject arbitrary headers (e.g., BCC, additional recipients, or even a second MIME body). The `Subject` is formed from `msg.Title` + `msg.Version`, which come from release data that is ultimately upstream-sourced.

**Recommendation:** Strip or reject `\r` and `\n` characters from all values interpolated into MIME headers. A minimal fix:

```go
func sanitizeHeader(s string) string {
    r := strings.NewReplacer("\r", "", "\n", "")
    return r.Replace(s)
}
```

Then wrap each header value: `sanitizeHeader(cfg.FromAddress)`, `sanitizeHeader(data.Subject)`, and sanitize each element of `cfg.ToAddresses`.

### 2. Context deadline not applied to SMTP operations

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.go`, lines 115-151

The design spec says "10-second timeout via context," but the implementation accepts a `context.Context` parameter and never uses it. The `net.Dialer{}` on line 121 has no timeout, and `smtp.Dial` on line 135 also has no timeout. If the SMTP server hangs, this goroutine blocks indefinitely, which will eventually starve the River worker pool.

The Slack and Discord senders get their timeout from `&http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}` set in `NewSenders()`. The `EmailSender` has no equivalent mechanism.

**Recommendation:** Use a `net.Dialer` with a context-derived deadline or an explicit timeout:

```go
dialer := &net.Dialer{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}
// For port 465:
conn, err := tls.DialWithDialer(dialer, "tcp", addr, ...)
// For STARTTLS:
conn, err := dialer.DialContext(ctx, "tcp", addr)
client, err = smtp.NewClient(conn, cfg.SMTPHost)
```

This also aligns the `EmailSender` with how the other senders handle timeouts.

---

## Important Issues (Should Fix)

### 3. `NewSenders()` doc comment is stale

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go`, line 42

```go
// NewSenders returns the default sender map for webhook, Slack, and Discord.
```

The comment still says "webhook, Slack, and Discord" but now includes `"email"`. This should be updated to mention all four channels.

### 4. HTML template does not escape `DownloadCommand` or `Changelog` for XSS in webmail clients

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.html.tmpl`, lines 37 and 45

```html
<pre ...>{{.Changelog}}</pre>
<code ...>{{.DownloadCommand}}</code>
```

Go's `html/template` auto-escapes `{{.Changelog}}` and `{{.DownloadCommand}}` in normal HTML contexts. However, inside `<pre>` and `<code>` tags, the content is still HTML-escaped by the `html/template` engine, so this is safe by default. This is **not** a vulnerability, but it is worth noting that if anyone switches to `text/template` in the future, this would become an XSS vector. A comment would be prudent.

### 5. The `html/template` escaping may mangle download commands

Because `html/template` escapes `&`, `<`, `>`, and `"`, a download command like `docker pull foo:bar --flag="value"` would render with `&quot;` in the HTML email. This is technically correct for HTML rendering but could look odd if an email client does unexpected things with pre-formatted code blocks. Worth a manual verification with a real email client.

### 6. Empty `ToAddresses` slice is not validated

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.go`, lines 68-71

If `to_addresses` is an empty JSON array `[]`, the code will call `client.Mail()` and then skip the `client.Rcpt()` loop entirely, then proceed to `client.Data()`. Most SMTP servers will reject this, but the error message would be opaque. An early validation with a clear error would be better:

```go
if len(cfg.ToAddresses) == 0 {
    return fmt.Errorf("email config: to_addresses is empty")
}
```

Similarly, `cfg.SMTPHost`, `cfg.FromAddress`, and `cfg.SMTPPort` should be validated.

---

## Suggestions (Nice to Have)

### 7. Mock SMTP server could be more robust

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email_test.go`, lines 23-78

The mock server uses raw `conn.Read(buf)` with a fixed 4096-byte buffer. If the SMTP client sends a command that takes two TCP segments (unlikely but possible under load), the mock could misparse. The `bufio.Scanner` or `textproto.Reader` would be more robust. This is unlikely to cause flaky tests in practice but worth noting.

### 8. No test for connection failure / SMTP error paths

The tests cover: happy path (source release), happy path (semantic report), invalid config, and multiple recipients. Missing coverage:

- SMTP server unreachable (connection refused)
- SMTP server rejects AUTH (bad credentials)
- SMTP server rejects a recipient (partial delivery)
- Template rendering failure (unlikely but possible with a nil pointer in data)

At minimum, a test for connection failure would be valuable since that is the most common operational error.

### 9. The `urgencyColor` function duplicates color constants from `discordUrgencyColor`

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.go`, lines 52-65

The hex color values in `urgencyColor()` match the Discord values in `discordUrgencyColor()` (e.g., `#DC2626` = `0xDC2626`). Consider extracting shared constants to avoid future drift if someone updates one but not the other. Not a bug today, but a maintenance risk.

### 10. QUIT error is silently swallowed at the caller level

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.go`, line 179

```go
return client.Quit()
```

If `client.Quit()` returns an error (e.g., network hiccup during the goodbye handshake), this propagates up to the River worker, which would retry the entire job. Since the email was already successfully delivered at this point (after `wc.Close()` succeeded), this could cause duplicate emails. Consider logging the `Quit()` error instead of returning it:

```go
if err := client.Quit(); err != nil {
    slog.Warn("smtp quit error (email already sent)", "err", err)
}
return nil
```

---

## Spec Compliance Summary

| Design Spec Requirement | Status |
|---|---|
| New file `email.go` implementing `Sender` | Implemented |
| New file `email.html.tmpl` embedded via `//go:embed` | Implemented |
| New file `email_test.go` | Implemented |
| One-line change in `worker.go` | Implemented |
| Config schema matches spec | Matches exactly |
| STARTTLS (port 587) support | Implemented |
| Direct TLS (port 465) support | Implemented |
| `PLAIN` auth with username/password | Implemented |
| Multipart HTML + plain text | Implemented |
| Semantic report with urgency badge, summary, download | Implemented |
| Source release fallback | Implemented |
| 10-second timeout via context | **NOT implemented** (Critical #2) |
| No database migration needed | Confirmed |

---

## Verdict

The implementation is well-structured and follows the existing codebase patterns closely. The two critical issues -- SMTP header injection and missing connection timeout -- should be addressed before merging. The header injection is a security concern, and the missing timeout is an operational reliability concern that deviates from the design spec. The important and suggestion items are lower priority but would improve the robustness of the implementation.

   182→func (s *EmailSender) buildEmailData(msg Notification) emailData {
   183→	data := emailData{
   184→		SourceURL:     msg.SourceURL,
   185→		ReleaseURL:    msg.ReleaseURL,
   186→		ProviderLabel: ProviderLabel(msg.Provider),
   187→		UrgencyColor:  "#2563EB", // default blue
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Footer
   191→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   192→		data.FooterText = fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Try semantic report first.
   196→	var report models.SemanticReport
   197→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   198→		urgency := report.Urgency
   199→		if urgency == "" {
   200→			urgency = report.RiskLevel
   201→		}
   202→		urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
   203→		if urgencyReason == "" {
   204→			urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
   205→		}
   206→
   207→		data.Subject = fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", msg.Title, msg.Version)
   208→		data.UrgencyColor = urgencyColor(urgency)
   209→		if urgency != "" {
   210→			data.UrgencyBadge = fmt.Sprintf("%s %s Urgency", urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)
   211→		}
   212→		upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
   213→		if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
   214→			data.UrgencyReason = urgencyReason
   215→		}
   216→		summary := report.ChangelogSummary
   217→		if summary == "" {
   218→			summary = report.Summary
   219→		}
   220→		data.Summary = summary
   221→		if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   222→			data.DownloadCommand = report.DownloadCommands[0]
   223→		}
   224→		return data
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	// Fallback: source-level release.
   228→	data.Subject = fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", msg.Title, msg.Version)
   229→	if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok && fields.Changelog != "" {
   230→		data.Changelog = markdownToASCII(fields.Changelog)
   231→	}
   232→	return data
   233→}
   234→
   235→func (s *EmailSender) buildPlainText(data emailData) string {
   236→	var b strings.Builder
   237→	b.WriteString(data.Subject + "\n")
   238→	b.WriteString(strings.Repeat("=", len(data.Subject)) + "\n\n")
   239→
   240→	if data.UrgencyBadge != "" {
   241→		b.WriteString(data.UrgencyBadge + "\n")
   242→		if data.UrgencyReason != "" {
   243→			b.WriteString(data.UrgencyReason + "\n")
   244→		}
   245→		b.WriteString("\n")
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	if data.Summary != "" {
   249→		b.WriteString(data.Summary + "\n\n")
   250→	}
   251→
   252→	if data.Changelog != "" {
   253→		b.WriteString(data.Changelog + "\n\n")
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	if data.DownloadCommand != "" {
   257→		b.WriteString("  " + data.DownloadCommand + "\n\n")
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	if data.SourceURL != "" {
   261→		b.WriteString("View on " + data.ProviderLabel + ": " + data.SourceURL + "\n")
   262→	}
   263→	if data.ReleaseURL != "" {
   264→		b.WriteString("View in Changelogue: " + data.ReleaseURL + "\n")
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	if data.FooterText != "" {
   268→		b.WriteString("\n---\n" + data.FooterText + "\n")
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	return b.String()
   272→}

> AGENT

All done. Here's the summary:

## What was implemented

**4 commits** adding email as a notification channel:

| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `internal/routing/email.html.tmpl` | HTML email template with inline CSS, urgency badges, changelog, download commands |
| `internal/routing/email.go` | `EmailSender` — SMTP delivery with STARTTLS/TLS, multipart MIME (HTML + plain text) |
| `internal/routing/email_test.go` | 4 tests with mock SMTP server (send, semantic report, invalid config, multiple recipients) |
| `internal/routing/worker.go` | One-line registration in `NewSenders()` |

**Post-review fixes applied:**
- SMTP header injection prevention (`sanitizeHeader`)
- 10-second dial timeout on SMTP connections
- Config validation (empty `to_addresses`)
- Safe `Quit()` handling (no duplicate emails on network hiccup)

**Usage:** Create a channel with `type: "email"` and config:
```json
{
  "smtp_host": "smtp.example.com",
  "smtp_port": 587,
  "username": "alerts@example.com",
  "password": "secret",
  "from_address": "no-reply@example.com",
  "to_addresses": ["team@example.com"]
}
```

> DEVELOPER

how to create a smtp server?

> AGENT

For testing locally, the easiest options:

**1. MailHog** (catches all emails, web UI to view them):
```bash
docker run -d -p 1025:1025 -p 8025:8025 mailhog/mailhog
```
- SMTP on port `1025` (no auth needed)
- Web UI at `http://localhost:8025`

**2. MailPit** (modern MailHog replacement):
```bash
docker run -d -p 1025:1025 -p 8025:8025 axllent/mailpit
```

**3. Python one-liner** (dumps emails to terminal):
```bash
python3 -m smtpd -n -c DebuggingServer localhost:1025
```

For the current implementation, which requires auth, MailHog/MailPit work best since they accept any credentials. Use this channel config:

```json
{
  "smtp_host": "127.0.0.1",
  "smtp_port": 1025,
  "username": "any",
  "password": "any",
  "from_address": "changelogue@localhost",
  "to_addresses": ["you@localhost"]
}
```

Note: the `EmailSender` currently requires auth (`smtp.PlainAuth`). These test servers accept any username/password, so it works as-is. If you need to use a server that doesn't support auth at all, that would need a code change.

## Summary

I found the frontend code that renders the "Add Channel" popup/modal in the Changelogue project. Here are the exact locations where channel types are defined and rendered:

### 1. **Channel Type Options (Frontend Form)**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/channels/channel-form.tsx`

**Lines 12-24** - The `channelFields` object defines the available channel types and their config fields:

```typescript
const channelFields: Record<string, { label: string; placeholder: string }[]> = {
  slack: [
    { label: "Webhook URL", placeholder: "https://hooks.slack.com/services/..." },
    { label: "Channel", placeholder: "#releases" },
  ],
  pagerduty: [
    { label: "Routing Key", placeholder: "R0xxxxx" },
  ],
  webhook: [

  ],
};
```

**Lines 84-90** - The Channel Type Select dropdown in the form:

```typescript

  <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
  <SelectContent>

    <SelectItem value="pagerduty">PagerDuty</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="webhook">Webhook</SelectItem>

```

### 2. **Channel Type Styling/Badges (Display)**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/channels/page.tsx`

**Lines 16-20** - The `TYPE_STYLES` object that defines colors and icons for each channel type:

```typescript
const TYPE_STYLES: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; icon: IconType }> = {

**Line 244** - The modal title and 245-250 - Where ChannelForm is rendered in the "Add Channel" dialog.

### 3. **Backend Supported Types**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go`

**Lines 42-49** - The `NewSenders()` function that already supports **"email"** as a channel type:

```go
func NewSenders() map[string]Sender {
	return map[string]Sender{
		"webhook": &WebhookSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
		"slack":   &SlackSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
		"discord": &DiscordSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
		"email":   &EmailSender{},
	}
}
```

### Key Findings

**Important:** The backend already fully supports "email" as a channel type (including `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/email.go` and tests), but it's **missing from the frontend**. The Discord type is also missing from the frontend form dropdowns, though it has styling defined.

To add "email" as an option, you need to:

1. Add `email` field definitions to `channelFields` in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/channels/channel-form.tsx` (line 12)
2. Add `<SelectItem value="email">Email</SelectItem>` in the type dropdown (line 89)
3. Add styling to `TYPE_STYLES` in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/channels/page.tsx` (line 16)

    34→export function ChannelForm({ initial, onSubmit, title, onSuccess, onCancel }: ChannelFormProps) {

    36→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

    45→    const key = label.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, "_");

    47→  };
    48→
    49→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    50→    e.preventDefault();
    51→    setError("");
    52→    setSaving(true);
    53→    try {
    54→      await onSubmit({ type, name, config });
    55→      if (onSuccess) {
    56→        onSuccess();
    57→      } else {
    58→        router.push("/channels");
    59→      }
    60→    } catch (err: unknown) {

    62→    } finally {
    63→      setSaving(false);
    64→    }
    65→  };
    66→
    67→  const handleCancel = () => {
    68→    if (onCancel) {

    70→    } else {
    71→      router.back();
    72→    }
    73→  };
    74→

    81→      </div>

   105→      </div>

   139→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   140→                        fontSize: "11px",
   141→                        fontWeight: 500,
   142→                        textTransform: "uppercase",
   143→                        letterSpacing: "0.08em",
   144→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   145→                        borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   146→                      }}
   147→                    >
   148→                      {heading}
   149→                    </th>
   150→                  )
   151→                )}
   152→              </tr>
   153→            </thead>
   154→            <tbody>
   155→              {channels.map((ch) => (
   156→                <tr
   157→                  key={ch.id}
   158→                  className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   159→                  style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   160→                >
   161→                  {/* Name */}
   162→                  <td
   163→                    className="px-5 py-3.5"
   164→                    style={{
   165→                      fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   166→                      fontSize: "14px",
   167→                      fontWeight: 500,
   168→                      color: "#111113",
   169→                    }}
   170→                  >
   171→                    {ch.name}
   172→                  </td>
   173→
   174→                  {/* Type */}
   175→                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5">
   176→                    <TypeBadge type={ch.type} />
   177→                  </td>
   178→
   179→                  {/* Config */}
   180→                  <td
   181→                    className="max-w-xs truncate px-5 py-3.5"
   182→                    style={{
   183→                      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   184→                      fontSize: "12px",
   185→                      color: "#6b7280",
   186→                    }}
   187→                  >
   188→                    {Object.entries(ch.config)
   189→                      .map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${String(v)}`)
   190→                      .join(", ")}
   191→                  </td>
   192→
   193→                  {/* Created */}
   194→                  <td
   195→                    className="px-5 py-3.5"
   196→                    style={{
   197→                      fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   198→                      fontSize: "13px",
   199→                      color: "#6b7280",
   200→                    }}
   201→                  >
   202→                    {new Date(ch.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
   203→                  </td>
   204→
   205→                  {/* Actions */}
   206→                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5">
   207→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   208→                      <button
   209→                        onClick={() => handleTest(ch)}
   210→                        disabled={testingId === ch.id}
   211→                        className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-amber-50 hover:text-amber-600 disabled:opacity-50"
   212→                        title="Send test notification"
   213→                      >
   214→                        {testingId === ch.id ? (
   215→                          <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
   216→                        ) : (
   217→                          <Zap className="h-4 w-4" />
   218→                        )}
   219→                      </button>
   220→                      <button
   221→                        onClick={() => setEditingChannel(ch)}
   222→                        className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1] hover:text-[#111113]"
   223→                      >
   224→                        <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
   225→                      </button>
   226→                      <button
   227→                        onClick={() => setDeletingId(ch.id)}
   228→                        className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   229→                      >
   230→                        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   231→                      </button>
   232→                    </div>
   233→                  </td>
   234→                </tr>
   235→              ))}
   236→            </tbody>
   237→          </table>
   238→        )}
   239→      </div>
   240→
   241→      {/* Create dialog */}
   242→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   243→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   244→          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Add Channel</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
   245→          <ChannelForm
   246→            title="Add Channel"
   247→            onSubmit={async (input) => { await channelsApi.create(input); }}
   248→            onSuccess={() => { setCreateOpen(false); mutate("channels"); }}
   249→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   250→          />
   251→        </DialogContent>
   252→      </Dialog>
   253→
   254→      {/* Edit dialog */}
   255→      <Dialog open={!!editingChannel} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setEditingChannel(null); }}>
   256→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   257→          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Edit Channel</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
   258→          {editingChannel && (
   259→            <ChannelForm
   260→              key={editingChannel.id}
   261→              title="Edit Channel"
   262→              initial={editingChannel}
   263→              onSubmit={async (input) => { await channelsApi.update(editingChannel.id, input); }}
   264→              onSuccess={() => { setEditingChannel(null); mutate("channels"); }}
   265→              onCancel={() => setEditingChannel(null)}
   266→            />
   267→          )}
   268→        </DialogContent>
   269→      </Dialog>
   270→
   271→      {/* Delete dialog */}
   272→      <ConfirmDialog
   273→        open={!!deletingId}
   274→        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setDeletingId(null); }}
   275→        title="Delete Channel"
   276→        description="This will permanently delete this notification channel. This cannot be undone."
   277→        onConfirm={async () => { if (deletingId) { await channelsApi.delete(deletingId); mutate("channels"); } }}
   278→      />
   279→
   280→      {/* Toast notifications */}
   281→      <ToastContainer toasts={toasts} onDismiss={dismissToast} />
   282→    </div>
   283→  );
   284→}
   285→

The config is sent as `Record<string, unknown>` — so the form stores raw values and sends them as JSON. The issue is that email needs `smtp_port` as a number and `to_addresses` as an array. I need to handle the type conversion in the form's submit handler.

The simplest approach: add email fields to `channelFields`, and add a config transform in `handleSubmit` that converts the email-specific fields to proper types. Let me make the changes.

> AGENT

Confirmed. Two issues:

1. **`smtp.PlainAuth` refuses to authenticate over unencrypted connections** — Go's stdlib enforces this as a security measure. MailHog doesn't support TLS by default.
2. **MailHog doesn't actually _deliver_ emails** — it's a local mail trap. Even if the connection worked, emails would only show up in MailHog's web UI (port 8025), not in your real Gmail inbox.

Let me fix issue #1 (skip auth when credentials are empty) so it works with local test servers like MailHog:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_vrtx_01TkzYDUFyEMmBdkJhKCKi3m

  "new_string": "\t// Only authenticate when credentials are provided. This allows\n\t// unauthenticated local servers like MailHog/MailPit for testing.\n\tif cfg.Username != \"\" || cfg.Password != \"\" {\n\t\tauth := smtp.PlainAuth(\"\", cfg.Username, cfg.Password, cfg.SMTPHost)\n\t\tif err := client.Auth(auth); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"smtp auth: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}"
}

> AGENT

Two things were wrong:

**1. Auth failure** — Go's `smtp.PlainAuth` refuses to send credentials over unencrypted connections. MailHog runs plain SMTP on port 1025 with no TLS. Fixed: credentials are now skipped when username/password are empty. Configure your MailHog channel with empty credentials:

```json
{
  "smtp_host": "127.0.0.1",
  "smtp_port": 1025,
  "username": "",
  "password": "",
  "from_address": "changelogue@localhost",
  "to_addresses": ["pooytr1@gmail.com"]
}
```

**2. MailHog doesn't deliver to real inboxes** — MailHog is a local mail trap. It captures emails but never forwards them. Your email will appear at `http://localhost:8025`, not in Gmail.

To actually receive email at `pooytr1@gmail.com`, you'd need a real SMTP relay like Gmail SMTP, AWS SES, or Mailgun.

> DEVELOPER

then locally how can i test the email?

> AGENT

Open MailHog's web UI at **http://localhost:8025** — that's where all captured emails show up. It acts as a fake inbox.

To test:

1. Make sure MailHog is running: `docker run -d -p 1025:1025 -p 8025:8025 mailhog/mailhog`
2. Create/update your email channel with empty username/password
3. Hit the test button (lightning bolt) on the channel row
4. Open **http://localhost:8025** — you should see the email there with full HTML rendering

MailHog shows you exactly what a real recipient would see — subject, HTML body, plain text alternative, headers — without needing a real mail server.

     1→<p align="center">

     4→
     5→![Build](https://github.com/sentioxyz/releaseguard/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)
     6→![AI Co-Authored](https://img.shields.io/endpoint?url=https://gist.githubusercontent.com/Poytr1/94cc8f0ddf90bb1d04dafbb76102786d/raw/ai-commits.json)
     7→
     8→## What it does
     9→
    10→- **Discovers releases** by polling Docker Hub and GitHub on configurable intervals
    11→- **Routes notifications** to Slack, Discord, and webhooks the moment a new version lands
    12→- **Generates AI reports** via Google Gemini agents that research changelogs, assess risk, and summarize what changed
    13→- **Serves a dashboard** (Next.js) for managing projects, sources, subscriptions, and browsing releases in real time
    14→
    15→## Architecture
    16→
    17→```mermaid
    18→graph LR
    19→    A["Ingestion<br/>(polling)"] --> B["PostgreSQL +<br/>River Queue"]
    20→    B --> C["Agent<br/>(ADK-Go)"]
    21→    C --> D["Routing<br/>(channels)"]
    22→
    23→    A -.- A1["Docker Hub<br/>GitHub"]
    24→    B -.- B1["Transactional Outbox<br/>LISTEN/NOTIFY → SSE"]
    25→    C -.- C1["Gemini LLM<br/>research tools"]
    26→    D -.- D1["Slack · Discord<br/>Webhooks"]
    27→```
    28→
    29→Release insert and job enqueue happen in a single SQL transaction — zero-loss guarantee.
    30→
    31→See [ARCH.md](ARCH.md), [API.md](API.md), and [DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md) for the full design.
    32→
    33→## Quick start
    34→
    35→**Prerequisites:** Go 1.25+, Docker, Node.js 20+
    36→
    37→```bash
    38→# Start Postgres and the server (NO_AUTH mode)
    39→make dev
    40→
    41→# In another terminal — start the frontend
    42→make frontend-install
    43→make frontend-dev
    44→```
    45→
    46→The API runs on `localhost:8080`, the dashboard on `localhost:3000`.
    47→
    48→## Environment variables
    49→
    50→| Variable | Default | Purpose |
    51→|----------|---------|---------|
    52→| `DATABASE_URL` | `postgres://localhost:5432/changelogue?sslmode=disable` | PostgreSQL connection |
    53→| `LISTEN_ADDR` | `:8080` | HTTP server bind address |
    54→| `NO_AUTH` | _(unset)_ | Set to `true` to disable API key auth (development) |
    55→| `LOG_LEVEL` | `info` | Log level: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error` |
    56→| `LLM_PROVIDER` | `gemini` | LLM provider: `gemini` or `openai` |
    57→| `LLM_MODEL` | `gemini-2.5-flash` / `gpt-5.2` | Model name (default depends on provider) |
    58→| `GOOGLE_API_KEY` | _(empty)_ | Gemini API key (required when `LLM_PROVIDER=gemini`) |
    59→| `OPENAI_API_KEY` | _(empty)_ | OpenAI API key (required when `LLM_PROVIDER=openai`) |
    60→| `OPENAI_BASE_URL` | `https://api.openai.com/v1` | OpenAI-compatible API base URL |
    61→
    62→## Project structure
    63→
    64→```
    65→cmd/
    66→  server/              Entry point — wires all layers together
    67→  agent/               Agent CLI — run agent analysis for a project
    68→internal/
    69→  agent/               ADK-Go agent orchestrator, tools, and worker
    70→    openai/            OpenAI-compatible LLM provider
    71→  api/                 REST API, SSE, middleware, auth
    72→  db/                  Connection pool and migrations
    73→  ingestion/           Polling sources (Docker Hub, GitHub Atom)
    74→  models/              Shared domain types
    75→  queue/               River job definitions and client
    76→  routing/             Notification channels and delivery worker
    77→web/                   Next.js dashboard (React + Tailwind + shadcn)
    78→scripts/               Integration test harness
    79→```
    80→
    81→## Extending
    82→
    83→More providers (npm, PyPI, Helm, etc.) and channels (PagerDuty, email, etc.) are planned. Adding one is a single-interface implementation:
    84→
    85→**Add a registry provider** — implement `IIngestionSource` in `internal/ingestion/source.go`:
    86→
    87→```go
    88→type IIngestionSource interface {
    89→    FetchNewReleases(ctx context.Context) ([]IngestionResult, error)
    90→}
    91→```
    92→
    93→**Add a notification channel** — implement `Sender` in `internal/routing/sender.go`:
    94→
    95→```go
    96→type Sender interface {
    97→    Send(ctx context.Context, channel models.NotificationChannel, msg Message) error
    98→}
    99→```
   100→

   102→

   104→make build              # go build -o changelogue ./cmd/server
   105→make test               # go test ./...
   106→make vet                # go vet ./...
   107→make lint               # alias for vet
   108→make integration-test   # full integration test (spins up its own Postgres)
   109→make db-reset           # drop and recreate the local database
   110→make frontend-build     # build Next.js static export
   111→make agent-dev          # run agent CLI for a specific project
   112→make clean              # remove binary, stop containers, delete volumes
   113→```
